     - 2026-05-20 t140 — Mini progress bar inside the keyboard page-nav toast. The t132 "📄 Page N / M" toast that pulses bottom-center after every keyboard page-nav keypress (`[` `]` from t70, `Home` `End` from t128, `{` `}` from t136) gave an exact page NUMBER but no spatial sense of WHERE in the now-~70-page / 1,740+-entry library you actually landed — "Page 41 / 70" reads as a number you have to mentally divide to feel. This adds a thin 3px progress track directly under the toast's page-number label, with a white fill whose width equals `(currentPage - 1) / (totalPages - 1)` as a percentage — so a `}` 10-page jump or an `End` press now lands with the fill snapping to its new depth, giving an at-a-glance "I'm about 60% through the grid" read that a bare number never conveyed. The 0-indexed formula means page 1 shows an empty track and the last page shows a full track (the true endpoints), and the fill animates with a 280ms cubic-bezier width transition so rapid `]` `]` `]` presses visibly slide the bar forward rather than teleporting it — the same coalescing the t132 toast already does for the label. Implementation is deliberately minimal and reuses every existing mechanism: the track + fill are PERMANENT children of the single lazily-created `#libpage-toast` element (built once on first use inside the same `if (!pt)` block that creates the toast, reused on every subsequent call — so still zero DOM on cold load, exactly like the toast itself), the page-number text moved into a `#libpage-toast-label` child div (the toast was previously bare `textContent`), and only the label text + the fill's `width` change per call. Percentage is computed from the SAME post-clamp `window.__libCurrentPage` / `window.__libTotalPages` geometry t132 publishes from `paint()`, so it always reflects the real clamped page; a single-page grid (`totalPages === 1`) harmlessly shows a full bar via a guarded `tot > 1` ternary. Toast corner-radius softened from a 99px pill to a 14px rounded rectangle so the inner track has room to breathe; padding nudged 1px to seat the bar. No new keybind, no new top-level DOM node, no network, no change to any handler, pager, filter, or to `showPageToast()`'s call sites — the four/six page-nav keys all benefit for free since they already route through `showPageToast()`. Mobile-friendly by inaction (touch users never emit page-nav keys, so the toast never renders on phones), hidden-on-print inheritance unchanged. Pairs with t132 (the toast it lives inside) and t136 (the 10-page jump it most helps orient) — completes the keyboard page-nav feedback story: t132 told you the page, t140 shows you the position. Edit on disk, will ship at next scheduled deploy.
     - 2026-05-20 t136 — `{` / `}` keyboard shortcuts jump 10 pages at once across the library grid. The library is now ~70 pages / 1,740+ entries, and the existing keyboard page-nav set only offered single-step moves (`[` prev / `]` next from t70) or the two endpoints (`Home` first / `End` last from t128) — nothing in between. Skimming from, say, page 12 to page 40 meant either 28 `]` presses or jumping to `End` and walking backwards. This adds the natural fast-skip rung: `}` (Shift+`]`) advances 10 pages, `{` (Shift+`[`) retreats 10 pages, so the same 28-page hop is now three keypresses instead of 28. Implementation is deliberately minimal and reuses every existing mechanism: both cases live inside the SAME t70/t76/t128 keydown handler block (added directly after the `[` case), so they inherit all its guards for free — ignored while typing in INPUT/TEXTAREA/contentEditable, ignored when Ctrl/Meta/Alt is held (note: `{`/`}` are produced by Shift, NOT a guarded modifier, so they pass the guard cleanly while `[`/`]` stay distinct). `}` sets `currentPage += 10` and leans on the exact upper-bound clamp trick the `]` handler already documents ("paint() clamps to totalPages"), so `}` near the end of the grid harmlessly lands on the last page with no `totalPages` reference needed in the handler scope; `{` sets `currentPage = Math.max(1, currentPage - 10)` since `paint()` only clamps the upper bound, so `{` near the start harmlessly lands on page 1. Both then call `paint()`, fire the t132 `showPageToast()` so the visitor gets the same "📄 Page N / M" bottom-center confirmation pill the other four page-nav keys produce (a 10-page jump is exactly the case where you most need to know where you landed), and smooth-scroll the viewport to the top — behavior identical to `[`/`]`/`Home`/`End`. A no-op press (`}` already on the last page, `{` on page 1) re-confirms the current page via the toast rather than misreporting. Shortcuts overlay (t57/t100) updated with a "Jump 10 pages back / forward" row directly under the existing "First page / Last page" row in the Navigation section, same kbd styling. Mobile-friendly by inaction — touch users never emit `{`/`}`, so zero visual impact on phones. Pure additive: no existing handler, function, pager, filter, or state changed; ~16 lines for the two cases + 1 overlay row, zero new DOM, zero new network, zero permanent DOM. Completes the keyboard page-nav family — t70 single-step `[`/`]`, t128 endpoints `Home`/`End`, t136 the missing 10-page coarse-skip in between — with t132's toast giving consistent landing feedback across all six keys. Edit on disk, will ship at next scheduled deploy.
     - 2026-05-20 t132 — Transient "📄 Page N / M" toast on keyboard page-navigation. The four keyboard page-nav shortcuts (`[` prev / `]` next from t70, `Home` first / `End` last from t128) all smooth-scroll the viewport to the very top on every press — which means the ONLY on-screen "Page N / M" readout, the uppercase label baked into the bottom pager (`buildPagerHtml()`), is never in view at the moment you actually land. Until now, keyboard page-nav was effectively blind: you could mash `]` or hit `End` and have no idea whether you'd moved, hit the last page, or which page you arrived on, short of scrolling all the way back down to the pager. This adds a small pulse-orange pill that pulses in bottom-center for ~1.1s after each page-nav keypress, reading e.g. "📄 Page 12 / 70". Implementation deliberately clones the proven t108 min-qs toast pattern verbatim — a single lazily-created `#libpage-toast` element (created once on first use, reused thereafter, zero DOM on cold load), opacity fade in/out via a 180ms transition, auto-hide on a `clearTimeout`-guarded 1.1s timer so rapid `]` `]` `]` presses coalesce into one continuously-visible toast that re-confirms the latest page rather than stacking. The toast reads the REAL post-clamp page geometry: `paint()` now publishes the clamped values to `window.__libCurrentPage` / `window.__libTotalPages` right after the existing `currentPage` clamp (two assignment lines, no logic change), so the handler — which lives outside `paint()` scope and historically had no `totalPages` reference (the t128 comment explicitly notes this) — can finally show an accurate total without re-deriving it. This means `End` on the current ~70-page / 1,740-entry grid correctly reads "Page 70 / 70", and a no-op press (`]` on the last page, `Home` on page 1) harmlessly re-confirms the current page rather than misreporting. The toast carries `role="status"` + `aria-live="polite"` so screen-reader users get the same page-change confirmation sighted users do — a small but real accessibility win the silent scroll-jump never provided. `showPageToast()` is a plain function in the same scope as the `[`/`]`/`Home`/`End` handlers and is called immediately after each `paint()`; the keydown block inherits all its existing guards for free (ignored while typing in INPUT/TEXTAREA/contentEditable, ignored when Ctrl/Meta/Alt held). Mobile-friendly by inaction — touch users never emit `[`/`]`/`Home`/`End`, so zero visual impact on phones. Pure additive: no existing handler, function, pager, or filter behavior changed; ~22 lines for the helper + 2 lines in `paint()` + 4 one-line `showPageToast()` calls, zero new network, zero permanent DOM. Pairs naturally with the t108 min-qs toast (same bottom-center pill language) and completes the keyboard page-nav trio (t70 `[`/`]`, t128 `Home`/`End`, t132 the visible feedback all four were missing). Edit on disk, will ship at next scheduled deploy.
     - 2026-05-20 t128 — `Home` / `End` keybinds jump to the FIRST / LAST page of the library grid. Completes the page-navigation keyboard pattern started by t70 (`[` previous page / `]` next page) with the two missing endpoints: `Home` snaps straight to page 1, `End` snaps to the final page. Until now, getting from a deep page back to the start meant repeated `[` presses (or the t83 `t` / ↑ Top button, which scrolls but does NOT change the page), and there was no single-keystroke path to the LAST page at all — a real gap given the library is now 1,740+ entries spread across ~70 pages at the current PAGE_SIZE. Both keys live inside the SAME existing keydown handler as `[` / `]` (the t70/t76 block), so they inherit its guards for free: ignored when typing in INPUT/TEXTAREA/contentEditable, ignored when Ctrl/Meta/Alt is held. Implementation is deliberately minimal and reuses existing mechanism — `Home` sets `currentPage = 1` then calls `paint()`; `End` sets `currentPage = 1e9` then calls `paint()`, leaning on the exact clamp trick the `]` handler already documents ("paint() clamps to totalPages"), so no `totalPages`/`filtered` reference is needed in the handler scope. Both then smooth-scroll to the top of the viewport, matching `[` / `]` behavior precisely. Pressing `Home` while already on page 1 (or `End` on the last page) is a harmless no-op — paint() re-clamps. Shortcuts overlay (t57/t100) updated with a "First page / Last page" row directly under the existing "Previous page / Next page" row in the Navigation section, same kbd styling. Mobile-friendly by inaction — touch users never emit Home/End, so zero visual impact on phones. Pure additive: no existing handler, function, or state changed; ~14 lines, zero new DOM, zero network. Pairs naturally with the rest of the keyboard culture (j/k cards, [/] pages, g/y/s/c filters, 1-5 quality, r/n/o jumps, t Top, ? overlay). Edit on disk, will ship at next scheduled deploy.
     - 2026-05-19 t124 — In-flight rung bonus on the verified bar. Each polish rung-walk event (qs N→N+1 where N+1<10) adds +0.1pp to the displayed verified %, fetched from /.netlify/functions/pulse-activity-feed (7d window, refresh 60s). Label appends " · +N.N in flight" when bonus active. Pairs with v15 polish-rung cron — every 5-min tick now visibly nudges the bar.
     - 2026-05-19 t120 — Sticky filter chip row on /knowledge. As soon as the visitor scrolls past the hero + search-bar (window.scrollY > 360px), a slim fixed-top mini-bar slides down from the top of the viewport carrying: a compact ⌕ search input (two-way synced with the main #q-search), the three filter pills (★ Starred / ✨ Today / 🌟 Gold) mirroring their parent's live state + counts + on/off styling, and an ↑ Top mini-button (mirrors the t83 floating top button, here so it lives in the same chrome row). The bar is hidden by default and animates in via translateY + opacity (140ms cubic-bezier) — no layout shift, no jank. The mini ⌕ search proxies keystrokes BOTH WAYS: typing in the sticky bar updates #q-search.value and dispatches its 'input' event (so all existing filter logic fires unchanged); typing in the main search syncs back to the sticky field via the existing paint() hook. The three filter pills are clones rendered into the sticky bar; clicks are delegated to the original buttons via .click() so toggling Gold here flips the exact same window.__showGoldOnly state — single source of truth. State sync (active gradient, count text, hidden-when-zero) is refreshed on every paint() call via a new syncStickyFilterBar() helper. Scroll handler is passive + rAF-throttled (single requestAnimationFrame per tick, no debounce timer). Why useful: the library is now 1,740+ entries — visitors who deep-scroll on page 1 (≈25 cards) used to lose ALL filter chrome the moment they got past viewport ~600px, meaning every starred/gold/today toggle or new search required scrolling back to the top. Power-users (operator + return-visitor cohort) get instant access to filters from any scroll depth. Pairs naturally with: (a) t83 ↑ Top button (sticky bar's ↑ chip is a low-friction near-finger duplicate that doesn't require reaching for the bottom-right corner), (b) t70 [/] page nav (sticky bar makes mid-page filter toggles painless without losing position — though page nav scrolls to top anyway), (c) t89 `s` / t76 `g`/`y` / t108 `1-5` keybinds (sticky bar is the click-driven mirror of the keyboard culture, so mouse users get the same one-action filter access).     /* ─── t146 — BOLD ENERGIZED FILTER PILLS ──────────────────────────────────
       The three library-scope filter pills (★ Starred / ✨ Today / 🌟 Gold) were
       flat, dim, translucent rounded chips that read as dead chrome the moment
       they appeared — easy to miss entirely, and giving no obvious signal of
       which filter was currently ON. This turns the trio into an unmistakable
       energized control set: each pill now sits on a real glassy 3D face with a
       thick glowing pulse-orange border and a continuously BREATHING orange glow
       halo so the row pulses gently in your peripheral vision; hovering a pill
       lifts it, scales it up, fires a bright orange flare, and sweeps a diagonal
       light-sheen edge to edge; and the ACTIVE pill (whichever filter is toggled
       on) ignites — a permanent bright orange glow ring + a faster, stronger
       pulse — so "this filter is ON" is obvious at a glance from anywhere on the
       page. Purely additive + decorative: it deliberately overrides ONLY
       border / box-shadow / transform and adds ::before/::after layers — it
       NEVER touches `background` or `color`, which the toggle JS owns inline
       (active-state detected via the `color:#fff`/`color:#000` the handlers set,
       and via the `today-only` gradient), so the existing filter logic is
       untouched. No markup change, no JS, no new DOM, no network, no layout
       shift (transform/glow only). Respects prefers-reduced-motion, flattened on
       print, and the same treatment is mirrored onto the t120 sticky-bar pills
       so deep-scroll visitors get the identical energized read. */

    /* ─── t150 — BOLD STAGGERED CARD-ENTRANCE CASCADE ─────────────────────────
       Every prior tick decorated chrome or a hover/idle state — but the grid
       itself still SNAPPED into existence: open the page, change a filter,
       flip a pager button, and 25 cards just appeared instantly, dead-still.
       This makes the library visibly COME ALIVE every time it paints: each
       card now drops in with a real entrance — it rises 26px, fades up from
       0, un-blurs, and overshoots slightly into place — and the cards fire in
       a rapid top-to-bottom STAGGER so the page assembles itself in a fluid
       cascade you cannot miss. Because paint() rebuilds the `.entry` markup
       fresh on every render (initial load, every search keystroke, every
       filter toggle, every page change), this CSS-only animation REPLAYS on
       each of those — so the grid re-cascades whenever it changes, turning a
       static slab into a live, kinetic surface. Implemented purely as a
       keyframe on `.entry` plus per-position `nth-child` animation-delays
       (first 14 cards staggered ~46ms apart, the rest land together so a deep
       page never feels slow). Purely additive + CSS-only: no markup change,
       no JS, no network, no layout shift (transform/opacity only, and the
       final keyframe state is the natural resting state so nothing is left
       displaced). Respects prefers-reduced-motion (animation removed entirely
       — cards appear instantly), and is fully neutralized on print. */
    /* t163 — entrance cascade kept but made subtle: a small one-time fade-up,
       no blur, no scale, no overshoot, and only a brief stagger across the
       first few cards so the grid settles in quietly rather than cascading. */
    @keyframes entryCascadeIn {
      0%   { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(8px); }
      100% { opacity: 1; transform: translateY(0); }
    }
    .lib .entry {
      animation: entryCascadeIn 0.32s ease-out both;
    }
    .lib .entry:nth-child(1)  { animation-delay: 0.00s; }
    .lib .entry:nth-child(2)  { animation-delay: 0.03s; }
    .lib .entry:nth-child(3)  { animation-delay: 0.06s; }
    .lib .entry:nth-child(4)  { animation-delay: 0.09s; }
    .lib .entry:nth-child(5)  { animation-delay: 0.12s; }
    .lib .entry:nth-child(n+6) { animation-delay: 0.15s; }
    /* Search mode — all matches render at once; skip per-card entrance (hundreds of cards). */
    .lib.lib-search-all .entry { animation: none !important; opacity: 1 !important; transform: none !important; }
    .kn-search-scroll-hint {

    /* ─── t166 — RESTORED LEFT-TO-RIGHT GRID LIGHT-UP SWEEP ───────────────────
       The user explicitly asked for "the flashing lights that went out from
       left to right on the knowledge grid" — the staggered card light-up the
       t150 cascade carried before t163 calmed it. This brings back a SWEEP:
       when the grid paints, a brief pulse-orange glow fires across the cards
       one after another in sequence, then settles. Because paint() rebuilds
       every `.entry` fresh on each render (initial load, search, filter, page
       change), this CSS-only animation REPLAYS on each of those — once per
       render, never a perpetual loop. The glow lives on an `.entry::after`
       overlay (additive — does not touch card content or the t153/t142 layers)
       and is keyed by `nth-child` so each successive card lights ~70ms after
       the one before it, producing a clean ripple down the grid. The keyframe
       ends fully transparent so nothing is left lit. Tasteful: a single soft
       orange flash per card, not relentless flashing. Respects
       prefers-reduced-motion (no flash) and is neutral/hidden on print. */
    @keyframes entryFlashSweep {
      0%   { opacity: 0; }
      35%  { opacity: 1; }
      100% { opacity: 0; }
    }
    .lib .entry::after {
      content: "";
      position: absolute;
      inset: -1px;
      border-radius: 9px;
      pointer-events: none;
      z-index: 1;
      opacity: 0;
      background: linear-gradient(100deg,
        rgba(255,140,26,0) 0%,
        rgba(255,140,26,0.16) 45%,
        rgba(255,215,64,0.22) 55%,
        rgba(255,140,26,0) 100%);
      box-shadow: 0 0 22px rgba(255,140,26,0.34),
                  inset 0 0 14px rgba(255,179,30,0.18);
      animation: entryFlashSweep 0.62s ease-out 1 both;
    }
    .lib .entry:nth-child(1)::after  { animation-delay: 0.05s; }
    .lib .entry:nth-child(2)::after  { animation-delay: 0.12s; }
    .lib .entry:nth-child(3)::after  { animation-delay: 0.19s; }
    .lib .entry:nth-child(4)::after  { animation-delay: 0.26s; }
    .lib .entry:nth-child(5)::after  { animation-delay: 0.33s; }
    .lib .entry:nth-child(6)::after  { animation-delay: 0.40s; }
    .lib .entry:nth-child(7)::after  { animation-delay: 0.47s; }
    .lib .entry:nth-child(8)::after  { animation-delay: 0.54s; }
    .lib .entry:nth-child(9)::after  { animation-delay: 0.61s; }
    .lib .entry:nth-child(10)::after { animation-delay: 0.68s; }
    .lib .entry:nth-child(11)::after { animation-delay: 0.75s; }
    .lib .entry:nth-child(12)::after { animation-delay: 0.82s; }
    .lib .entry:nth-child(13)::after { animation-delay: 0.89s; }
    .lib .entry:nth-child(14)::after { animation-delay: 0.96s; }
    .lib .entry:nth-child(15)::after { animation-delay: 1.03s; }
    .lib .entry:nth-child(n+16)::after { animation-delay: 1.10s; }
    @media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
      .lib .entry::after { animation: none !important; display: none !important; }
    }
    @media print {
      .lib .entry::after { animation: none !important; display: none !important; }
    }

    /* ─── t155 — BOLD IGNITED CARD QUESTION TITLES ────────────────────────────
       Every prior tick lit up the chrome AROUND the cards — borders, hover
       glow, accent stripes, entrance cascade — but the single most important
       thing on each card, the QUESTION TEXT itself, was still flat 1.05rem
       bold ink: the exact element a visitor is actually trying to READ read
       as the dimmest part of a now very lively card. This gives the title
       real presence and makes it impossible to miss:
         • the title is bumped up (1.2rem, weight 800, tighter tracking) and
           filled with a bright orange→gold→cream gradient that perpetually
           shimmers edge-to-edge, so the question text itself glows;
         • a glowing pulse-orange "Q" gem is injected as a ::before marker at
           the head of every question — a bold, instantly-scannable bullet
           that breathes a soft orange halo so the whole grid reads as a
           column of live question prompts;
         • hovering a card IGNITES its title — the gradient flares brighter,
           the shimmer speeds up, a drop-shadow glow blooms, and a thin
           animated gradient underline-rail wipes in beneath the question.
       Purely additive + CSS-only: it targets the EXISTING `.entry-question`
       element (the `↗ open page` link inside it is explicitly reset back to
       its own flat color + given its own stacking context so the gradient
       text-fill never bleeds onto it), no markup change, no JS, no network,
       no layout shift (the "Q" gem reserves its own inline space; the
       underline-rail is an absolutely-positioned ::after). Pending visitor
       cards (.visitor-pending) keep their hot-pink identity — the gradient
       fill + Q gem are suppressed there so t69's pink treatment is untouched.
       Respects prefers-reduced-motion (animations off, static legible fill),
       flattened to plain ink on print, and the title scales down cleanly on
       mobile. */
    .entry-question {
      font-size: 1.2rem;
      font-weight: 800;
      letter-spacing: -0.012em;
      position: relative;
    }
    /* glowing "Q" gem marker at the head of every question */
    .entry:not(.visitor-pending) > .entry-question::before {

    }
    /* the question TEXT itself — static warm gradient fill (t163: the

    .entry:not(.visitor-pending) > .entry-question {

    .entry:not(.visitor-pending) > .entry-question::after {

    /* keep the inner `↗ open page` link readable — it must NOT inherit the

    .entry-question > a {

      color: rgba(255,140,26,0.85);

    /* ─── t166 — TOP 10 MOST VIEWED THIS WEEK ─────────────────────────────────
       A clean ranked list of the 10 most-read library entries over the last 7
       days, mounted high on the page (above the LIVE RESEARCH BEACON). The 7-day read counts come from the
       entry-view function's ?all=1 endpoint, which the page ALREADY fetches
       into `readCounts` to drive the per-card 🔥 reads badge — so this widget
       is a pure reuse of in-memory data with NO new network request. The
       entry-view blob stores per-entry per-day buckets, and ?all=1 returns the
       genuine rolling sum of the last 7 calendar days, so this is a TRUE
       last-7-days top 10, not an all-time list. Calm + on-theme (dark glass,
       pulse-orange/gold), no perpetual animation, mobile-friendly, hidden on
       print. Hidden entirely when there is no read data yet. */
    #kn-top10 {
      position: relative;

         • a huge bold gradient title, "THE KNOWLEDGE GRID", filled with a
           live orange→gold→cream shimmer that perpetually sweeps edge-to-edge
           and carries a warm drop-shadow glow so it reads as lit, not flat;
         • a trio of pulse-orange "»»»" chevrons that march/glow toward the
           title, pulling the eye straight into the grid;
         • two glowing horizontal energy rails that flank the title left and
           right, each capped by a bright comet of light that perpetually
           races outward — so the whole band reads as a live circuit;
         • a small "live · cited · compounding" subcaption under the title;
         • the band breathes a soft orange halo so it pulses gently in
           peripheral vision.
       Purely additive + CSS-only: ONE new decorative, aria-hidden element
       inserted just before `<div class="lib" id="lib">` — no JS, no network,
       no layout coupling (it does not touch the grid, the cards, or any
       handler). Respects prefers-reduced-motion (all motion frozen, title
       stays a static legible gradient) and is fully hidden on print. */
    #kn-grid-header {
      position: relative;
      max-width: 1240px;
      margin: 30px auto 4px;
      padding: 4px clamp(20px, 5vw, 56px);
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      gap: clamp(12px, 3vw, 30px);
    }
    /* t163 — grid-header kept as a clean section divider, but the racing
       comet rails, the breathing core halo, the marching chevrons, and the
       perpetual title shimmer are all removed. Static, calm banner. */
    #kn-grid-header .kgh-rule {
      position: relative;
      flex: 1 1 auto;
      height: 1px;
      border-radius: 99px;
      background: linear-gradient(90deg,
        rgba(255,140,26,0) 0%,
        rgba(255,140,26,0.5) 50%,
        rgba(255,140,26,0.6) 100%);
    }
    #kn-grid-header .kgh-rule:last-child {
      background: linear-gradient(90deg,
        rgba(255,140,26,0.6) 0%,
        rgba(255,140,26,0.5) 50%,
        rgba(255,140,26,0) 100%);
    }
    /* the centre core — chevrons + title + subcaption */
    #kn-grid-header .kgh-core {
      flex: 0 0 auto;
      display: flex;
      flex-direction: column;
      align-items: center;
      gap: 3px;
      padding: 6px clamp(14px, 3vw, 26px);
      border-radius: 14px;
    }
    /* static pulse-orange chevrons pointing toward the title */
    #kn-grid-header .kgh-chevrons {
      font-size: 0.78rem;
      font-weight: 900;
      letter-spacing: 0.32em;
      color: rgba(255,157,46,0.65);
      line-height: 1;
    }
    /* the gradient title — static fill (t163: shimmer sweep removed) */
    #kn-grid-header .kgh-title {
      font-size: clamp(1.2rem, 3.4vw, 2rem);
      font-weight: 900;
      letter-spacing: 0.14em;
      line-height: 1.05;
      background: linear-gradient(100deg,
        #FF8C1A 0%, #FFD740 35%, #FFF7E6 50%, #FFD740 65%, #FF8C1A 100%);
      background-size: 100% 100%;
      -webkit-background-clip: text; background-clip: text;
      -webkit-text-fill-color: transparent; color: transparent;
    }
    /* small subcaption under the title */
    #kn-grid-header .kgh-sub {
      font-size: 0.56rem;
      font-weight: 800;
      letter-spacing: 0.3em;
      text-transform: uppercase;
      color: rgba(255,205,150,0.7);
    }
    @media (max-width: 640px) {
      #kn-grid-header { margin-top: 22px; gap: 10px; }
      #kn-grid-header .kgh-chevrons { font-size: 0.66rem; }
      #kn-grid-header .kgh-sub { letter-spacing: 0.2em; }
    }
    @media print {
      #kn-grid-header { display: none !important; }
    }

  <!-- Cleanup progress bar — shows how many entries the layout-cleanup pass has touched.
       Hides itself when not running (no _cleanup_progress.json blob, or done). -->
  <div id="cleanup-bar" style="position:fixed;top:0;left:0;right:0;height:36px;z-index:9998;background:rgba(7,10,15,0.95);border-bottom:1px solid rgba(57,255,20,0.35);align-items:center;gap:14px;padding:0 16px;font-family:'JetBrains Mono','SF Mono',monospace;font-size:0.64rem;letter-spacing:0.16em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#B6FFB4;backdrop-filter:blur(8px);">
    <span style="font-weight:900;color:#39FF14;white-space:nowrap;">🧹 Cleanup Pass</span>
    <div style="flex:1;height:8px;background:rgba(57,255,20,0.08);border:1px solid rgba(57,255,20,0.25);border-radius:99px;overflow:hidden;min-width:80px;">
      <div id="cb-fill" style="height:100%;width:0%;background:linear-gradient(90deg,#39FF14 0%,#00B830 100%);border-radius:99px;box-shadow:0 0 10px rgba(57,255,20,0.55);transition:width 0.7s cubic-bezier(0.22,1,0.36,1);"></div>
    </div>
    <span id="cb-pct" style="font-weight:900;color:#39FF14;min-width:46px;text-align:right;">—%</span>
    <span id="cb-detail" style="opacity:0.75;font-size:0.58rem;white-space:nowrap;">— / — cleaned · now: <span id="cb-now">—</span></span>
  </div>
  <style>body { padding-top: 36px; } #cleanup-bar { display: flex !important; } #cleanup-bar.hidden { display: none !important; } body.no-cleanup-bar { padding-top: 0; }</style>
  <script>
  (function(){
    function poll(){
      fetch('/.netlify/functions/pulse-cleanup-status', { cache:'no-store' })
        .then(function(r){ return r.ok ? r.json() : null; })
        .then(function(d){
          var bar = document.getElementById('cleanup-bar');
          if (!bar) return;
          if (!d || !d.ok || !d.active) {
            bar.classList.add('hidden');
            document.body.classList.add('no-cleanup-bar');
            return;
          }
          bar.classList.remove('hidden');
          document.body.classList.remove('no-cleanup-bar');
          var done = d.done || 0, total = d.total || 1, pct = (done/total)*100;
          var fill = document.getElementById('cb-fill');
          var pctEl = document.getElementById('cb-pct');
          var detail = document.getElementById('cb-detail');
          var nowEl = document.getElementById('cb-now');
          if (fill) fill.style.width = Math.min(100,pct).toFixed(1)+'%';
          if (pctEl) pctEl.textContent = pct.toFixed(1)+'%';
          if (detail) detail.firstChild.textContent = done+' / '+total+' cleaned · now: ';
          if (nowEl) nowEl.textContent = d.current || '—';
          if (done >= total) {
            setTimeout(function(){ bar.classList.add('hidden'); document.body.classList.add('no-cleanup-bar'); }, 8000);
          }
        })
        .catch(function(){});
    }
    poll(); setInterval(poll, 4000);
  })();
  </script>
  <script>
  (function(){
    var label = document.getElementById('hn-label');
    var text  = document.getElementById('hn-text');
    var tgt   = document.getElementById('hn-target');
    var dot   = document.getElementById('hn-dot');
    function paintIdle(){
      if (label) { label.textContent = 'IDLE'; label.style.color = '#7BB6FF'; label.style.textShadow = '0 0 8px rgba(123,182,255,0.6)'; }
      if (dot)   { dot.style.background = '#7BB6FF'; dot.style.boxShadow = '0 0 10px rgba(123,182,255,0.6)'; }
      if (text)  text.textContent = 'standing by';
      if (tgt)   tgt.textContent = '';
    }
    function paintActive(action, target, score){
      var actionMap = {
        writing:   { color:'#FF3996', glow:'rgba(255,57,150,0.85)', label:'NOW WRITING' },
        polishing: { color:'#FFB347', glow:'rgba(255,179,71,0.85)',  label:'NOW POLISHING' },
        indexing:  { color:'#39FF14', glow:'rgba(57,255,20,0.85)',   label:'NOW INDEXING' },
        auditing:  { color:'#7C3AED', glow:'rgba(124,58,237,0.9)', label:'NOW AUDITING' },
        idle:      { color:'#7BB6FF', glow:'rgba(123,182,255,0.6)',  label:'IDLE' },
      };
      var m = actionMap[action] || actionMap.writing;
      if (label) { label.textContent = m.label; label.style.color = m.color; label.style.textShadow = '0 0 8px ' + m.glow; }
      if (dot)   { dot.style.background = m.color; dot.style.boxShadow = '0 0 12px ' + m.glow + ',0 0 4px ' + m.color; }
      var textMsg = action === 'writing' ? 'baseline + tldr + core + flow'
                  : action === 'polishing' ? ('layer ' + (score || '?') + '/10')
                  : action === 'indexing' ? 'pinging Bing / Yandex'
                  : action === 'auditing' ? 'double-checking 10/10 entries'
                  : action === 'idle' ? ('last finished ' + (target || ''))
                  : '—';
      if (text) text.textContent = textMsg;
      if (tgt)  tgt.textContent = target ? ('· ' + target) : '';
    }
    // Live in-flight card highlight — paints a bright green glow on the cards
    // for ids actively being rewritten right now by Claude Opus subagents (up
    // to 4 in parallel). Rotates as agents finish and the next ones start.
    function paintInFlightCards(){
      document.querySelectorAll('.entry.is-live-polishing').forEach(function(c){
        c.classList.remove('is-live-polishing');
      });
    }
    // t164 — Live "now auditing" throb. Paints a gentle purple pulse on the one
    // card the auditor process is currently checking (audit_target from
    // ?status=1). Empty target = nobody auditing → clear every card.
    function paintAuditingCard(){
      document.querySelectorAll('.entry.is-live-auditing').forEach(function(c){
        c.classList.remove('is-live-auditing');
      });
    }
    function refresh(){
      fetch('/.netlify/functions/pulse-machine-library-list?status=1', { cache: 'no-store' })
        .then(function(r){ return r.ok ? r.json() : null; })
        .then(function(d){
          if (!d || !d.ok) return;
          // Hot Now bar
          var a = d.current_activity;
          if (a) {
            var ageMs = Date.now() - (a.ts || 0);
            if (ageMs > 5 * 60 * 1000) paintActive('idle', a.target);
            else paintActive(a.action || 'writing', a.target, a.score);
          } else paintIdle();
          // Live card highlights — rotate as agents start/finish
          paintInFlightCards();
          paintAuditingCard();
        })
        .catch(function(){ /* silent */ });
    }
    refresh();
    setInterval(refresh, 4000);
  })();
  </script>

  <style>
    #kwww-stage{position:fixed;top:50%;left:50%;pointer-events:none;z-index:2;opacity:0;width:280px;
      transform:translate(-50%,-50%) scale(0.92);transition:opacity 1.2s ease,transform 1.2s ease,left 3.5s ease,top 3.5s ease;}
    #kwww-stage.kwww-swoop{transition:opacity 1.2s ease,transform 1.2s ease,left 3.5s ease,top 3.5s ease;}

    #kwww-stage.ps-show-photo #kwww-flash{display:block;}

    #kwww-stage.ps-glitch #kwww-flash{filter:blur(0.2px) saturate(1.4);}
    #kwww-deco{position:absolute;inset:0;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;pointer-events:none;}
    #kwww-deco .ps-card{width:100%;text-align:center;padding:18px 14px;border-radius:14px;
      background:linear-gradient(145deg,rgba(12,16,22,0.94),rgba(28,18,8,0.9));
      border:2px solid rgba(232,113,10,0.45);box-shadow:0 12px 40px rgba(0,0,0,0.55),0 0 24px rgba(232,113,10,0.12);}
    #kwww-deco .ps-orb{font-size:4.2rem;line-height:1;color:#E8710A;text-shadow:0 0 28px rgba(232,113,10,0.55);}
    #kwww-deco .ps-whisper{font-size:0.82rem;font-weight:700;color:rgba(237,229,216,0.92);line-height:1.45;letter-spacing:0.02em;}
    #kwww-deco .ps-label{font-size:0.58rem;font-weight:900;letter-spacing:0.2em;color:rgba(255,180,80,0.75);margin-bottom:8px;}
    @keyframes kwww-pop{0%,100%{transform:translate(-50%,-50%) scale(1);}50%{transform:translate(-50%,-50%) scale(1.06);}}
    @keyframes kwww-glitch{0%,100%{clip-path:inset(0 0 0 0);}33%{clip-path:inset(8% 0 62% 0);transform:translate(calc(-50% + 4px),-50%);}66%{clip-path:inset(55% 0 5% 0);transform:translate(calc(-50% - 4px),-50%);}}
    @media(prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){#kwww-stage{display:none!important;}}
  </style>
  <div id="kwww-stage" aria-hidden="true">
    <img id="kwww-flash" src="/assets/kwww.jpg" alt="" loading="lazy">
    <div id="kwww-deco"></div>

  <script>
  (function(){
    var stage = document.getElementById('kwww-stage');
    var img   = document.getElementById('kwww-flash');
    var deco  = document.getElementById('kwww-deco');
    if (!stage || !deco || !img) return;
    if (window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches) return;

    var whispers = [
      'Coverage without stage hygiene is theater.',
      'The board reads commit — not weighted pipe.',
      'One field change beats a new dashboard.',
      'RevOps is CRM truth, not slide polish.',
      'Handoffs fail in fields, not meetings.',
      'Sandbagging is a forecast process bug.',
      'Your next hire might be a routing rule.',
      'NRR lives in expansion fields — tag them.',
      'MEDDPICC without dates is decoration.',
      'The Machine never sleeps. You might.',
      'Press / to search. Press g for gold.',
      'Surprise: you found the library easter egg.'
    ];
    var hats = ['🎩','👑','🎃','🎅','🍀','😎','🤠','🫡','⛑️'];
    var kinds = [
      { id:'mystery', w:38 },
      { id:'whisper', w:32 },
      { id:'signal',  w:18 },
      { id:'silhouette', w:7 },
      { id:'cameo',   w:4 },
      { id:'glitch',  w:1 }
    ];

    function pick(arr){ return arr[Math.floor(Math.random()*arr.length)]; }
    function pickKind(){
      var r = Math.random() * 100, acc = 0;
      for (var i = 0; i < kinds.length; i++) {
        acc += kinds[i].w;
        if (r < acc) return kinds[i].id;
      }
      return 'mystery';
    }

    function flash(){
      var kind = pickKind();
      var top  = 10 + Math.random() * 78;
      var left = 10 + Math.random() * 78;
      var size = kind === 'whisper' ? 240 + Math.random() * 80 : 160 + Math.random() * 120;
      var hold = kind === 'whisper' ? 3200 + Math.random() * 1200 : 2200 + Math.random() * 1400;

      stage.className = '';
      stage.style.animation = '';
      stage.style.width = size + 'px';
      stage.style.top = top + '%';
      stage.style.left = left + '%';
      stage.style.opacity = '0';
      stage.style.transform = 'translate(-50%,-50%) scale(0.9)';

      if (kind === 'mystery') {
        deco.innerHTML = '<div class="ps-card"><div class="ps-label">SURPRISE</div><div class="ps-orb">?</div></div>';
      } else if (kind === 'whisper') {
        deco.innerHTML = '<div class="ps-card"><div class="ps-label">WHISPER</div><p class="ps-whisper">' + whispers[Math.floor(Math.random()*whispers.length)] + '</p></div>';
      } else if (kind === 'signal') {
        deco.innerHTML = '<div class="ps-card"><div class="ps-label">PULSE SIGNAL</div><div class="ps-orb">◉</div></div>';
      } else {
        stage.classList.add('ps-show-photo');
        if (kind === 'silhouette') stage.classList.add('ps-silhouette');
        if (kind === 'glitch') stage.classList.add('ps-glitch');
        var hat = '<span style="position:absolute;top:2%;left:50%;transform:translateX(-50%);font-size:'+(size*0.42|0)+'px">' + pick(hats) + '</span>';
        deco.innerHTML = kind === 'cameo' ? hat : (kind === 'glitch' ? '' : '');
      }

      requestAnimationFrame(function(){
        stage.style.opacity = kind === 'silhouette' ? '0.22' : (kind === 'cameo' ? '0.3' : '0.42');
        stage.style.transform = 'translate(-50%,-50%) scale(1)';
        if (kind === 'glitch') stage.style.animation = 'kwww-glitch 420ms steps(2) 3';
        else if (Math.random() < 0.35) stage.style.animation = 'kwww-pop 1.1s ease-in-out 2';
        if (Math.random() < 0.25) {
          stage.classList.add('kwww-swoop');
          stage.style.left = ((left + (Math.random() < 0.5 ? 22 : -22) + 100) % 88 + 6) + '%';
        }
      });

      setTimeout(function(){
        stage.style.animation = '';
        stage.style.opacity = '0';
        stage.classList.remove('kwww-swoop','ps-show-photo','ps-silhouette','ps-glitch');
        deco.innerHTML = '';
      }, hold);
    }

    function schedule(){
      if (Math.random() < 0.12) { setTimeout(schedule, (14 + Math.random() * 18) * 60 * 1000); return; }
      flash();
      setTimeout(schedule, (14 + Math.random() * 16) * 60 * 1000);
    }
    setTimeout(schedule, 90000 + Math.random() * 120000);
  })();
  </script>

  <script>
  // Auto-populate the Sales Trainings count badge in the top nav so visitors
  // see the pillar is alive and compounding (not an empty new tab). Hidden
  // gracefully if the count is 0 or the fetch fails — the link still works.
  (function(){
    var badge = document.getElementById('kn-st-count');
    if (!badge) return;
    fetch('/.netlify/functions/pulse-machine-library-list?pillar=training&recent=5000', { cache: 'no-store' })
      .then(function(r){ return r.ok ? r.json() : null; })
      .then(function(j){
        var n = j && Array.isArray(j.entries) ? j.entries.length : 0;
        if (n > 0) { badge.textContent = n; badge.style.display = 'inline-block'; }
      })
      .catch(function(){ /* silent */ });
  })();
  // Same for the Industry KPIs pillar count badge.
  (function(){
    var badge = document.getElementById('kn-ik-count');
    if (!badge) return;
    fetch('/.netlify/functions/pulse-machine-library-list?pillar=kpi&recent=500', { cache: 'no-store' })
      .then(function(r){ return r.ok ? r.json() : null; })
      .then(function(j){
        var n = j && Array.isArray(j.entries) ? j.entries.length : 0;
        if (n > 0) { badge.textContent = n; badge.style.display = 'inline-block'; }
      })
      .catch(function(){ /* silent */ });
  })();

      .kn-up-pill.t-cron-crm{background:rgba(34,197,94,0.14);color:#22c55e;border:1px solid rgba(34,197,94,0.4);}

      .kn-up-pill.t-cron-polish{background:rgba(255,179,71,0.14);color:#FFB347;border:1px solid rgba(255,179,71,0.35);}
      .kn-up-pill.t-cron-salestraining{background:rgba(255,215,64,0.14);color:#FFD740;border:1px solid rgba(255,215,64,0.4);}
      .kn-up-title{font-size:0.78rem;color:rgba(237,229,216,0.78);margin-top:3px;line-height:1.35;overflow:hidden;display:-webkit-box;-webkit-line-clamp:2;-webkit-box-orient:vertical;}

  <script>
  (function(){
    var btn      = document.getElementById('kn-updates-btn');
    var sheet    = document.getElementById('kn-updates-sheet');
    var panel    = document.getElementById('kn-updates-panel');
    var backdrop = document.getElementById('kn-updates-backdrop');
    var closeBtn = document.getElementById('kn-updates-close');
    var bodyEl   = document.getElementById('kn-updates-body');
    var sumEl    = document.getElementById('kn-updates-summary');
    var topCount = document.getElementById('kn-updates-count');
    var rangeRow = document.getElementById('kn-updates-rangerow');
    if (!btn || !sheet) return;

    var range = 'today';
    var lastFirstTs = 0;
    var refreshTimer = null;
    var isOpen = false;
    var seenIds = new Set();

    function pad(n){ return n < 10 ? '0'+n : ''+n; }
    function fmtTime(ms){
      var d = new Date(ms);
      return pad(d.getHours()) + ':' + pad(d.getMinutes());
    }
    function fmtHourBucket(ms){
      var d = new Date(ms);
      var hr = d.getHours();
      var lbl = (hr === 0 ? '12' : (hr > 12 ? hr-12 : hr)) + (hr < 12 ? ' AM' : ' PM');
      return d.toLocaleDateString(undefined, { month:'short', day:'numeric' }) + ' · ' + lbl;
    }
    function escapeHtml(s){
      return String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function(c){
        return { '&':'&amp;', '<':'&lt;', '>':'&gt;', '"':'&quot;', "'":'&#39;' }[c];
      });
    }
    function render(j){
      if (!j || !j.events || !j.events.length){
        bodyEl.innerHTML = '<div class="kn-up-empty">No activity yet for this range — check back in a few minutes, the Machine is always on.</div>';
        sumEl.innerHTML = '';
        if (topCount) topCount.style.display = 'none';
        return;
      }
      var c = j.counts || {};
      sumEl.innerHTML =
        '<span style="background:rgba(167,139,250,0.14);color:#a78bfa;border:1px solid rgba(167,139,250,0.4);padding:3px 9px;border-radius:99px;">'+ (c.cron || 0) +' cron ticks</span>' +
        '<span style="background:rgba(255,140,26,0.14);color:#FF8C1A;border:1px solid rgba(255,140,26,0.4);padding:3px 9px;border-radius:99px;">'+ (c.created || 0) +' published</span>' +
        '<span style="background:rgba(34,197,94,0.14);color:#22c55e;border:1px solid rgba(34,197,94,0.4);padding:3px 9px;border-radius:99px;">'+ (c.verified || 0) +' verified 10/10</span>' +
        '<span style="background:rgba(255,179,71,0.14);color:#FFB347;border:1px solid rgba(255,179,71,0.35);padding:3px 9px;border-radius:99px;">'+ (c.polish || 0) +' polish ticks</span>' +
        '<span style="background:rgba(57,255,20,0.10);color:#39FF14;border:1px solid rgba(57,255,20,0.32);padding:3px 9px;border-radius:99px;">'+ (c.index || 0) +' index pings</span>';
      if (topCount){ topCount.textContent = c.total || j.events.length; topCount.style.display = 'inline-block'; }
      // Group by hour bucket.
      var out = [];
      var lastBucket = '';
      j.events.forEach(function(ev){
        var b = fmtHourBucket(ev.ts);
        if (b !== lastBucket){ out.push('<div class="kn-up-hour">'+ escapeHtml(b) +'</div>'); lastBucket = b; }
        var fresh = (ev.ts > lastFirstTs && lastFirstTs > 0 && !seenIds.has(ev.id+'_'+ev.ts)) ? ' is-fresh' : '';
        seenIds.add(ev.id+'_'+ev.ts);
        var idHtml = ev.id ? ('<span class="kn-up-id">'+ escapeHtml(ev.id) +'</span>') : '';
        var title  = ev.url
          ? '<a href="'+ escapeHtml(ev.url) +'">'+ escapeHtml(ev.title || '') +'</a>'
          : escapeHtml(ev.title || '');
        out.push(
          '<div class="kn-up-row'+ fresh +'">'+
            '<div class="kn-up-time">'+ fmtTime(ev.ts) +'</div>'+
            '<div class="kn-up-dot t-'+ ev.type +'" aria-hidden="true"></div>'+
            '<div class="kn-up-main">'+
              '<div class="kn-up-label"><span class="kn-up-pill t-'+ ev.type +'">'+ escapeHtml(ev.label || ev.type) +'</span>'+ idHtml +'</div>'+
              '<div class="kn-up-title">'+ title +'</div>'+
            '</div>'+
          '</div>'
        );
      });
      bodyEl.innerHTML = out.join('');
      lastFirstTs = j.events[0].ts;
    }
    function load(){
      fetch('/.netlify/functions/pulse-activity-feed?day=' + encodeURIComponent(range) + '&limit=600', { cache: 'no-store' })
        .then(function(r){ return r.ok ? r.json() : null; })
        .then(function(j){ if (j && j.ok) render(j); else bodyEl.innerHTML = '<div class="kn-up-empty">Feed not available right now. Try again in a moment.</div>'; })
        .catch(function(){ bodyEl.innerHTML = '<div class="kn-up-empty">Feed not available right now.</div>'; });
    }
    function open(){
      if (isOpen) return; isOpen = true;
      sheet.style.display = 'block';
      sheet.setAttribute('aria-hidden','false');
      requestAnimationFrame(function(){ panel.classList.add('kn-open'); });
      load();
      if (refreshTimer) clearInterval(refreshTimer);
      refreshTimer = setInterval(load, 60000);  // 60s while open (was 20s) — cut function load 3x
    }
    function close(){
      if (!isOpen) return; isOpen = false;
      panel.classList.remove('kn-open');
      setTimeout(function(){ sheet.style.display = 'none'; sheet.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true'); }, 280);
      if (refreshTimer){ clearInterval(refreshTimer); refreshTimer = null; }
    }
    btn.addEventListener('click', function(){ isOpen ? close() : open(); });
    closeBtn.addEventListener('click', close);
    backdrop.addEventListener('click', close);
    document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e){
      if (e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey || e.altKey) return;
      var t = e.target, tag = (t && t.tagName || '').toUpperCase();
      if (tag === 'INPUT' || tag === 'TEXTAREA' || (t && t.isContentEditable)) return;
      if (e.key === 'Escape' && isOpen) { e.preventDefault(); close(); }
      else if (e.key === 'u') { e.preventDefault(); isOpen ? close() : open(); }
    });
    rangeRow.addEventListener('click', function(e){
      var b = e.target.closest('.kn-up-range');
      if (!b) return;
      range = b.getAttribute('data-range');
      rangeRow.querySelectorAll('.kn-up-range').forEach(function(x){
        var on = x === b;
        x.classList.toggle('kn-up-range-active', on);
        x.style.background = on ? 'rgba(255,215,64,0.18)' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.03)';
        x.style.borderColor = on ? 'rgba(255,215,64,0.5)' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.12)';
        x.style.color = on ? '#FFE082' : 'rgba(237,229,216,0.6)';
      });
      lastFirstTs = 0; seenIds.clear();
      load();
    });
    // Lightweight badge poll while closed — show today's count so visitors
    // know there's activity to peek at. Polls every 5 min when not open.
    function pollBadge(){
      if (isOpen) return;
      fetch('/.netlify/functions/pulse-activity-feed?day=today&limit=1', { cache: 'no-store' })
        .then(function(r){ return r.ok ? r.json() : null; })
        .then(function(j){
          if (j && j.ok && j.counts && j.counts.total > 0 && topCount){
            topCount.textContent = j.counts.total;
            topCount.style.display = 'inline-block';
          }
        })
        .catch(function(){});
    }
    pollBadge();
    setInterval(pollBadge, 900000);  // 15 min while closed (was 5 min) — cut function load 3x
  })();
  </script>

  <div class="hero">
    <div style="font-size:0.66rem;font-weight:800;letter-spacing:0.28em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--orange-bright);margin-bottom:10px;">◉ Posted (on site) — Cited Library</div>

For the raw working set (rapid-fire, processing queue), see <a href='/answers' style='color:var(--orange-bright);font-weight:700;'>Processing (not deployed)</a>.</p>

            <stop offset="1" stop-color="#FFF7E6"/>
          </linearGradient>
        </defs>
        <path class="kn-pm-echo" pathLength="1" d="M0 17 H60 l8 0 6 -9 7 18 6 -22 7 26 6 -13 H132 l8 0 6 -9 7 18 6 -22 7 26 6 -13 H204 l8 0 6 -9 7 18 6 -22 7 26 6 -13 H276 l8 0 6 -9 7 18 6 -10 H320"/>
        <path class="kn-pm-trace" pathLength="1" d="M0 17 H60 l8 0 6 -9 7 18 6 -22 7 26 6 -13 H132 l8 0 6 -9 7 18 6 -22 7 26 6 -13 H204 l8 0 6 -9 7 18 6 -22 7 26 6 -13 H276 l8 0 6 -9 7 18 6 -10 H320"/>
        <circle class="kn-pm-blip" r="3.2" cx="0" cy="0">
          <animateMotion dur="2.4s" repeatCount="indefinite"
            path="M0 17 H60 l8 0 6 -9 7 18 6 -22 7 26 6 -13 H132 l8 0 6 -9 7 18 6 -22 7 26 6 -13 H204 l8 0 6 -9 7 18 6 -22 7 26 6 -13 H276 l8 0 6 -9 7 18 6 -10 H320"/>

  <!-- ── Live quality ticker ─────────────────────────────────────────────
       Real-time breakdown of where every entry sits on the 5→10 quality

  <!-- Live Quality Ticker stats panel removed per owner request 2026-05-13. -->
  <section id="quality-ticker" hidden></section>
  <script>
  (function(){
    var els = {
      total: document.getElementById('qt-total'),
      queue: document.getElementById('qt-queue'),
      verified: document.getElementById('qt-verified'),
      indexRate: document.getElementById('qt-index-rate'),
      polishRate: document.getElementById('qt-polish-rate'),
      verifiedRate: document.getElementById('qt-verified-rate'),
      breakdown: document.getElementById('qt-breakdown'),
      updated: document.getElementById('qt-updated'),
    };
    function fmtNum(n){ return (n||0).toLocaleString('en-US'); }
    function refreshTicker(){
      fetch('/.netlify/functions/pulse-machine-library-list?status=1', {cache:'no-store'})
        .then(function(r){ return r.ok ? r.json() : null; })
        .then(function(d){
          if (!d || !d.ok) return;
          // Only two top bars remain: Index Progress and Claude Opus · Bespoke Rewrites.
          var ipbFill = document.getElementById('ipb-fill');
          var ipbPct  = document.getElementById('ipb-pct');
          var ipbDet  = document.getElementById('ipb-detail');
          if (ipbFill && d.library_total > 0) {
            var iCount = d.indexed_count || 0;
            var iPct = iCount / d.library_total * 100;
            ipbFill.style.width = Math.min(100, iPct).toFixed(2) + '%';
            if (ipbPct) ipbPct.textContent = iPct.toFixed(1) + '%';
            if (ipbDet) ipbDet.textContent = fmtNum(iCount) + ' / ' + fmtNum(d.library_total) + ' indexed';
          }
          // Claude Opus · Bespoke Rewrites progress bar
          var copbFill = document.getElementById('copb-fill');
          var copbPct  = document.getElementById('copb-pct');
          var copbDet  = document.getElementById('copb-detail');
          if (copbFill) {
            var co = d.claude_opus_progress || {};
            var coTotal = co.total_library || d.library_total || 0;
            var coDone = co.count || 0;
            var coPct = coTotal > 0 ? (coDone / coTotal * 100) : 0;
            copbFill.style.width = Math.min(100, coPct).toFixed(2) + '%';
            if (copbPct) copbPct.textContent = coPct.toFixed(2) + '%';
            if (copbDet) {
              var lastStr = co.last_id ? ' · latest: ' + co.last_id : '';
              copbDet.textContent = fmtNum(coDone) + ' / ' + fmtNum(coTotal) + ' rewritten' + lastStr;
            }
          }
          if (els.total) els.total.textContent = fmtNum(d.library_total);
          if (els.queue) els.queue.textContent = fmtNum(d.polishing_queue_depth);
          if (els.verified) els.verified.textContent = fmtNum(d.ten_of_ten_count);
          if (els.indexRate) els.indexRate.textContent = (d.run_rate_per_hour || 0).toFixed(1);
          if (els.polishRate) els.polishRate.textContent = (d.verified_rate_per_hour || 0).toFixed(1);
          if (els.verifiedRate) els.verifiedRate.textContent = (d.verified_rate_per_hour || 0).toFixed(1);
          // Last-write age — read most-recent ts from the index.
          var lastWriteEl = document.getElementById('qt-lastwrite');
          var lastWriteCard = document.getElementById('qt-lastwrite-card');
          if (lastWriteEl) {
            var lwTs = d.last_write_ms || 0;
            if (lwTs > 0) {
              var ageMin = Math.round((Date.now() - lwTs) / 60000);
              var label = ageMin < 1 ? 'just now' : ageMin < 60 ? (ageMin + 'm ago') : (Math.round(ageMin/60*10)/10 + 'h ago');
              lastWriteEl.textContent = label;
              if (ageMin > 90 && lastWriteCard) {
                lastWriteCard.style.background = 'rgba(255,60,40,0.12)';
                lastWriteCard.style.borderColor = '#ff6b3a';
                lastWriteEl.style.color = '#ff6b3a';
              } else if (lastWriteCard) {
                lastWriteCard.style.background = 'rgba(57,255,20,0.08)';
                lastWriteCard.style.borderColor = 'rgba(57,255,20,0.35)';
                lastWriteEl.style.color = '#7BFF8F';
              }
            } else {
              lastWriteEl.textContent = '—';
            }
          }
          var sb = d.score_breakdown || {};
          var pills = '';
          for (var s = 5; s <= 10; s++) {
            var c = sb[s] || 0;
            var pct = d.library_total > 0 ? Math.round(c * 100 / d.library_total) : 0;
            var tier = s >= 10 ? '#FFD740' : s >= 8 ? '#FFB347' : s >= 6 ? '#FF9D2E' : '#FF8C1A';
            pills += '<span style="display:inline-block;background:rgba(20,14,8,0.55);color:'+tier+';border:1px solid '+tier+';border-radius:99px;padding:2px 9px;">'+s+'/10: '+fmtNum(c)+' ('+pct+'%)</span>';
          }
          if (els.breakdown) els.breakdown.innerHTML = pills;
          var d2 = new Date();
          if (els.updated) els.updated.textContent = d2.toLocaleTimeString(undefined, {hour:'2-digit', minute:'2-digit', second:'2-digit'});
        })
        .catch(function(){});
    }
    refreshTicker();
    // Auto-refresh removed per owner request — visitor must reload manually.
  })();
  </script>

      <!-- t62 — density toggle: 📏 Compact / Cozy.  Persists to localStorage. -->

    <style>
      #q-random:hover { background: linear-gradient(135deg,rgba(167,139,250,0.32),rgba(232,113,10,0.18)) !important; color: #fff !important; border-color: rgba(167,139,250,0.8) !important; transform: translateY(-1px); }

      #q-surprise-gold:hover { background: linear-gradient(135deg,rgba(255,215,64,0.38),rgba(232,159,10,0.24)) !important; color: #fff !important; border-color: rgba(255,215,64,0.95) !important; transform: translateY(-1px); box-shadow: 0 0 14px rgba(255,215,64,0.45) !important; }
      #q-surprise-gold:active { transform: translateY(0); }
      @media print { #q-surprise-gold { display: none !important; } }
      /* t62 — density toggle CSS. When body has .density-compact the library
         grid shrinks: smaller question font, tighter padding, hidden meta row,
         compressed badge gaps. Lets power users see ~2x the cards on screen. */
      #q-density:hover { background: rgba(255,255,255,0.10) !important; color: #fff !important; border-color: rgba(255,255,255,0.28) !important; }

    <script>
      // t62 — density toggle (compact/cozy), persisted to localStorage.
      (function(){
        var KEY = 'pulse_density_v1';
        var btn = document.getElementById('q-density');
        var icon = document.getElementById('q-density-icon');
        var label = document.getElementById('q-density-label');
        if (!btn) return;
        function apply(mode){
          var compact = mode === 'compact';
          document.body.classList.toggle('density-compact', compact);
          if (label) label.textContent = compact ? 'Cozy' : 'Compact';
          if (icon) icon.textContent = compact ? '☰' : '📏';
          btn.title = compact ? 'Switch to cozy density' : 'Switch to compact density';
        }
        var saved = null;
        try { saved = localStorage.getItem(KEY); } catch(_) {}
        apply(saved === 'compact' ? 'compact' : 'cozy');
        btn.addEventListener('click', function(){
          var next = document.body.classList.contains('density-compact') ? 'cozy' : 'compact';
          try { localStorage.setItem(KEY, next); } catch(_) {}
          apply(next);
        });
        // Cross-tab sync — if user toggles in another tab, reflect here.
        window.addEventListener('storage', function(e){
          if (e.key !== KEY) return;
          apply(e.newValue === 'compact' ? 'compact' : 'cozy');
        });
      })();
    </script>
    <script>
    // 🎲 Random entry — fetches the live entry list and picks one at
    // random. API-driven so it works regardless of paint timing or
    // closure scope. Cached for 5min after first fetch to avoid repeated
    // hits if the visitor rolls multiple times.
    (function(){
      var btn = document.getElementById('q-random');
      if (!btn) return;
      var cache = null;
      var cacheTs = 0;
      function navigate(e) {
        if (!e || !e.id) return;
        var prefix = /^st\d+$/i.test(e.id) ? '/sales-trainings/' : '/knowledge/';
        window.location.href = prefix + e.id;
      }
      function pickAndGo() {
        var now = Date.now();
        if (cache && (now - cacheTs) < 5 * 60 * 1000) {
          navigate(cache[Math.floor(Math.random() * cache.length)]);
          return;
        }
        // Brief visual cue while fetching
        var origText = btn.innerHTML;
        btn.innerHTML = '<span style="font-size:1rem;line-height:1;">🎲</span><span>Rolling…</span>';
        fetch('/.netlify/functions/pulse-machine-library-list?recent=5000', { cache: 'force-cache' })
          .then(function(r){ return r.ok ? r.json() : null; })
          .then(function(j){
            btn.innerHTML = origText;
            if (!j || !Array.isArray(j.entries) || !j.entries.length) return;
            // Exclude training AND industry-kpi entries from this page's random
            // pool to match the page's main-feed filter (they live on their own
            // pillar pages — /sales-trainings and /industry-kpis).
            var pool = j.entries.filter(function(e){
              if (!e || !e.id) return false;
              if (/^st\d+$/i.test(e.id)) return false;
              if (/^ik\d+$/i.test(e.id)) return false;
              if (Array.isArray(e.tags) && e.tags.indexOf('sales-training') !== -1) return false;
              if (Array.isArray(e.tags) && e.tags.indexOf('industry-kpi') !== -1) return false;
              return true;
            });
            if (!pool.length) return;
            cache = pool;
            cacheTs = now;
            navigate(pool[Math.floor(Math.random() * pool.length)]);
          })
          .catch(function(){ btn.innerHTML = origText; });
      }
      btn.addEventListener('click', pickAndGo);
      document.addEventListener('keydown', function(ev){
        if (ev.ctrlKey || ev.metaKey || ev.altKey || ev.shiftKey) return;
        if (ev.key !== 'r') return;
        var t = ev.target, tag = (t && t.tagName || '').toUpperCase();
        if (tag === 'INPUT' || tag === 'TEXTAREA' || tag === 'SELECT') return;
        if (t && t.isContentEditable) return;
        ev.preventDefault();
        pickAndGo();
      });
    })();
    </script>
    <script>
    // t104 — ✨ Surprise me · gold. Same pattern as Random but restricted to
    // the gold-tier pool (qs=10 AND new format). Mirrors the canonical
    // hasNewFormat check used by the per-card gold pill (paint() line ~2125)
    // and the Gold filter pill (paint() line ~1925) so all three surfaces
    // agree on what counts as gold. Reuses the SAME force-cache fetch as
    // Random/Newest so subsequent presses are instant.
    (function(){
      var btn = document.getElementById('q-surprise-gold');
      if (!btn) return;
      var cache = null;
      var cacheTs = 0;
      function isGold(e){
        if (!e || !e.id) return false;
        if ((e.quality_score || 0) < 10) return false;
        var ids = String(e.id);
        if (/^st\d+$/i.test(ids)) return true;
        var qm = ids.match(/^q(\d+)$/);
        var qi = qm ? parseInt(qm[1], 10) : -1;
        if (e.format_v === '2026-05') return true;
        if (qi >= 9501) return true;
        if (qi >= 1946 && qi <= 1954) return true;
        if (Array.isArray(e.tags) && (
            e.tags.indexOf('revenue-fix') !== -1
            || e.tags.indexOf('cro-candidate-pitch') !== -1
            || e.tags.indexOf('executive-outreach') !== -1)) return true;
        return false;
      }
      function navigate(e){
        if (!e || !e.id) return;
        var prefix = /^st\d+$/i.test(e.id) ? '/sales-trainings/' : '/knowledge/';
        window.location.href = prefix + e.id;
      }
      function pickAndGo(){
        var now = Date.now();
        if (cache && (now - cacheTs) < 5 * 60 * 1000) {
          navigate(cache[Math.floor(Math.random() * cache.length)]);
          return;
        }
        var origText = btn.innerHTML;
        btn.innerHTML = '<span style="font-size:1rem;line-height:1;">✨</span><span>Rolling gold…</span>';
        fetch('/.netlify/functions/pulse-machine-library-list?recent=5000', { cache: 'force-cache' })
          .then(function(r){ return r.ok ? r.json() : null; })
          .then(function(j){
            btn.innerHTML = origText;
            if (!j || !Array.isArray(j.entries) || !j.entries.length) return;
            // Exclude training + industry-kpi entries (this page's pool) THEN
            // restrict to gold.
            var pool = j.entries.filter(function(e){
              if (!e || !e.id) return false;
              if (/^st\d+$/i.test(e.id)) return false;
              if (/^ik\d+$/i.test(e.id)) return false;
              if (Array.isArray(e.tags) && e.tags.indexOf('sales-training') !== -1) return false;
              if (Array.isArray(e.tags) && e.tags.indexOf('industry-kpi') !== -1) return false;
              return isGold(e);
            });
            // Defensive fallback: if no gold entries are loaded (cold cache /
            // brand-new library state), fall back to any q-id so the click
            // never silently no-ops.
            if (!pool.length) {
              pool = j.entries.filter(function(e){
                if (!e || !e.id) return false;
                if (/^st\d+$/i.test(e.id)) return false;
                if (/^ik\d+$/i.test(e.id)) return false;
                if (Array.isArray(e.tags) && e.tags.indexOf('sales-training') !== -1) return false;
                if (Array.isArray(e.tags) && e.tags.indexOf('industry-kpi') !== -1) return false;
                return true;
              });
            }
            if (!pool.length) return;
            cache = pool;
            cacheTs = now;
            navigate(pool[Math.floor(Math.random() * pool.length)]);
          })
          .catch(function(){ btn.innerHTML = origText; });
      }
      btn.addEventListener('click', pickAndGo);
      document.addEventListener('keydown', function(ev){
        if (ev.ctrlKey || ev.metaKey || ev.altKey) return;
        // Shift+G (capital G) — `g` alone toggles the Gold filter, so we use
        // the uppercase pairing for "jump to a random gold entry."
        if (ev.key !== 'G') return;
        var t = ev.target, tag = (t && t.tagName || '').toUpperCase();
        if (tag === 'INPUT' || tag === 'TEXTAREA' || tag === 'SELECT') return;
        if (t && t.isContentEditable) return;
        ev.preventDefault();
        pickAndGo();
      });
    })();
    </script>
    <script>
    // t96 — `n` keybind opens the NEWEST entry on the library. Mirrors `r`
    // random keybind but deterministic: picks the entry with the largest
    // `ts` (publish timestamp) in the same q/non-training pool that `r`
    // uses. Reuses the same /pulse-machine-library-list?recent=5000 fetch
    // with `force-cache`, so if the visitor already pressed `r` once the
    // newest pick is instant. Brief "Loading…" toast in the corner while
    // the first fetch resolves. Hidden when typing.
    (function(){
      var cache = null;
      var cacheTs = 0;
      function toast(msg, ms){
        var el = document.createElement('div');
        el.textContent = msg;
        el.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;bottom:78px;right:24px;z-index:9999;background:#15171c;border:1px solid rgba(232,113,10,0.5);color:#FFB04A;font:700 0.74rem ui-monospace,Menlo,Consolas,monospace;letter-spacing:0.04em;padding:8px 12px;border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0 4px 14px rgba(0,0,0,0.35);opacity:0;transition:opacity .18s ease;pointer-events:none;';
        document.body.appendChild(el);
        requestAnimationFrame(function(){ el.style.opacity = '1'; });
        setTimeout(function(){ el.style.opacity = '0'; setTimeout(function(){ el.remove(); }, 250); }, ms || 900);
      }
      function navigate(e){
        if (!e || !e.id) return;
        var prefix = /^st\d+$/i.test(e.id) ? '/sales-trainings/' : '/knowledge/';
        window.location.href = prefix + e.id;
      }
      function pickNewest(pool){
        if (!pool || !pool.length) return null;
        var best = pool[0], bestTs = +new Date(best.ts || best.published_at || 0) || 0;
        for (var i = 1; i < pool.length; i++){
          var t = +new Date(pool[i].ts || pool[i].published_at || 0) || 0;
          if (t > bestTs) { bestTs = t; best = pool[i]; }
        }
        return best;
      }
      function goNewest(){
        var now = Date.now();
        if (cache && (now - cacheTs) < 5 * 60 * 1000) {
          navigate(pickNewest(cache));
          return;
        }
        toast('🆕 Finding newest…', 1400);
        fetch('/.netlify/functions/pulse-machine-library-list?recent=5000', { cache: 'force-cache' })
          .then(function(r){ return r.ok ? r.json() : null; })
          .then(function(j){
            if (!j || !Array.isArray(j.entries) || !j.entries.length) return;
            var pool = j.entries.filter(function(e){
              if (!e || !e.id) return false;
              if (/^st\d+$/i.test(e.id)) return false;
              if (/^ik\d+$/i.test(e.id)) return false;
              if (Array.isArray(e.tags) && e.tags.indexOf('sales-training') !== -1) return false;
              if (Array.isArray(e.tags) && e.tags.indexOf('industry-kpi') !== -1) return false;
              return true;
            });
            if (!pool.length) return;
            cache = pool; cacheTs = now;
            navigate(pickNewest(pool));
          })
          .catch(function(){});
      }
      document.addEventListener('keydown', function(ev){
        if (ev.ctrlKey || ev.metaKey || ev.altKey || ev.shiftKey) return;
        if (ev.key !== 'n' && ev.key !== 'N') return;
        var t = ev.target, tag = (t && t.tagName || '').toUpperCase();
        if (tag === 'INPUT' || tag === 'TEXTAREA' || tag === 'SELECT') return;
        if (t && t.isContentEditable) return;
        // Skip if a modal that consumes letters is open (shortcuts overlay)
        var m = document.getElementById('kbd-help-modal');
        if (m && m.style.display === 'flex') return;
        ev.preventDefault();
        goNewest();
      });
    })();

    // t116 — `o` keybind opens the OLDEST entry on the library. Direct mirror
    // of t96 `n` newest, but picks the entry with the SMALLEST `ts` from the
    // same q/non-training pool. Reuses the same /pulse-machine-library-list
    // ?recent=5000 force-cache fetch — once `n` or `r` has fired this session
    // the oldest pick is instant. Hidden when typing or shortcuts overlay open.
    (function(){
      var cache = null;
      var cacheTs = 0;
      function toast(msg, ms){
        var el = document.createElement('div');
        el.textContent = msg;
        el.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;bottom:78px;right:24px;z-index:9999;background:#15171c;border:1px solid rgba(232,113,10,0.5);color:#FFB04A;font:700 0.74rem ui-monospace,Menlo,Consolas,monospace;letter-spacing:0.04em;padding:8px 12px;border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0 4px 14px rgba(0,0,0,0.35);opacity:0;transition:opacity .18s ease;pointer-events:none;';
        document.body.appendChild(el);
        requestAnimationFrame(function(){ el.style.opacity = '1'; });
        setTimeout(function(){ el.style.opacity = '0'; setTimeout(function(){ el.remove(); }, 250); }, ms || 900);
      }
      function navigate(e){
        if (!e || !e.id) return;
        var prefix = /^st\d+$/i.test(e.id) ? '/sales-trainings/' : '/knowledge/';
        window.location.href = prefix + e.id;
      }
      function pickOldest(pool){
        if (!pool || !pool.length) return null;
        var best = pool[0], bestTs = +new Date(best.ts || best.published_at || 0) || Infinity;
        for (var i = 1; i < pool.length; i++){
          var t = +new Date(pool[i].ts || pool[i].published_at || 0) || Infinity;
          if (t < bestTs) { bestTs = t; best = pool[i]; }
        }
        return best;
      }
      function goOldest(){
        var now = Date.now();
        if (cache && (now - cacheTs) < 5 * 60 * 1000) {
          navigate(pickOldest(cache));
          return;
        }
        toast('🕰 Finding oldest…', 1400);
        fetch('/.netlify/functions/pulse-machine-library-list?recent=5000', { cache: 'force-cache' })
          .then(function(r){ return r.ok ? r.json() : null; })
          .then(function(j){
            if (!j || !Array.isArray(j.entries) || !j.entries.length) return;
            var pool = j.entries.filter(function(e){
              if (!e || !e.id) return false;
              if (/^st\d+$/i.test(e.id)) return false;
              if (/^ik\d+$/i.test(e.id)) return false;
              if (Array.isArray(e.tags) && e.tags.indexOf('sales-training') !== -1) return false;
              if (Array.isArray(e.tags) && e.tags.indexOf('industry-kpi') !== -1) return false;
              return true;
            });
            if (!pool.length) return;
            cache = pool; cacheTs = now;
            navigate(pickOldest(pool));
          })
          .catch(function(){});
      }
      document.addEventListener('keydown', function(ev){
        if (ev.ctrlKey || ev.metaKey || ev.altKey || ev.shiftKey) return;
        if (ev.key !== 'o' && ev.key !== 'O') return;
        var t = ev.target, tag = (t && t.tagName || '').toUpperCase();
        if (tag === 'INPUT' || tag === 'TEXTAREA' || tag === 'SELECT') return;
        if (t && t.isContentEditable) return;
        var m = document.getElementById('kbd-help-modal');
        if (m && m.style.display === 'flex') return;
        ev.preventDefault();
        goOldest();
      });
    })();
    </script>
    <!-- Live result count — populated by paint() in app.js when the search
         filter is active. Hidden when search is empty so the layout doesn't
         shift. Sits flush under the input. -->
    <div id="q-result-count" style="display:none;font-size:0.7rem;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:0.08em;color:rgba(237,229,216,0.7);margin:6px 0 0 4px;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums;"></div>

    <style>
      /* Search-match highlighting inside the visible question text on each
         library card. Matches `<mark class="q-hit">…</mark>` injected by the
         render in app.js. Inherit text color but pop with the Pulse gold +
         a soft underline so the match reads as found, not as a button. */
      /* (main q-hit styles live in the global .entry-question mark.q-hit block above) */
    </style>
    <script>
    (function(){
      function isTyping(e){
        var tag = (e.target && e.target.tagName || '').toLowerCase();
        if (tag === 'input' || tag === 'textarea' || tag === 'select') return true;
        if (e.target && e.target.isContentEditable) return true;
        return false;
      }
      document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e){
        var s = document.getElementById('q-search');
        if (!s) return;
        if (e.key === '/' && !e.ctrlKey && !e.metaKey && !e.altKey) {
          if (isTyping(e)) return;
          var r = s.getBoundingClientRect();
          if (r.width === 0 || r.height === 0) return;
          e.preventDefault(); s.focus(); s.select(); return;
        }
        if (e.key === 'Escape' && document.activeElement === s) {
          if (s.value) {
            s.value = '';
            s.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
            e.preventDefault();
          } else {
            s.blur();
            e.preventDefault();
          }
        }
      }, true);
    })();

    // ── j/k card navigation + Enter to open (t32) ────────────────────────
    // Vim/Gmail/Linear pattern — j moves focus to next card, k to previous,
    // Enter opens the focused entry. Mirrors the CRM deal-table behavior
    // from tick 14. Skipped when typing in an input (covered by isTyping).
    // Visual cue is a gold left-bar + outline on the focused card via the
    // .kn-card-focus class; auto-scrolls into view if off-screen.
    (function(){
      var idx = -1;  // currently-focused card index in the visible NodeList
      function cards() {
        return Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('#lib .hub-card[data-id], #lib .entry[data-id]'));
      }
      function clearFocus() {
        document.querySelectorAll('.kn-card-focus').forEach(function(el){ el.classList.remove('kn-card-focus'); });
      }
      function setFocus(newIdx, all) {
        var list = all || cards();
        if (!list.length) { idx = -1; clearFocus(); return; }
        idx = Math.max(0, Math.min(list.length - 1, newIdx));
        clearFocus();
        var el = list[idx];
        el.classList.add('kn-card-focus');
        // Scroll into view with a comfortable margin from the sticky header
        var r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
        var top = r.top + window.scrollY;
        var pad = 96;
        if (r.top < pad || r.bottom > window.innerHeight - 24) {
          window.scrollTo({ top: top - pad, behavior: 'smooth' });
        }
      }
      function openFocused() {
        var list = cards();
        if (idx < 0 || idx >= list.length) return;
        var el = list[idx];
        // Prefer the explicit "open page" link if present; otherwise click the card.
        if (el.tagName === 'A' && el.href) { window.location.href = el.href; return; }
        var link = el.querySelector('a[href^="/knowledge/"]');
        if (link) { link.click(); return; }
        el.click();
      }
      function isTypingTarget(e) {
        var t = e.target, tag = (t && t.tagName || '').toUpperCase();
        if (tag === 'INPUT' || tag === 'TEXTAREA' || tag === 'SELECT') return true;
        if (t && t.isContentEditable) return true;
        return false;
      }
      document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e){
        if (e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey || e.altKey || e.shiftKey) return;
        if (isTypingTarget(e)) return;
        if (e.key === 'j') { e.preventDefault(); setFocus(idx < 0 ? 0 : idx + 1); }
        else if (e.key === 'k') { e.preventDefault(); setFocus(idx < 0 ? 0 : idx - 1); }
        else if (e.key === 'Enter' && idx >= 0) { e.preventDefault(); openFocused(); }
      });
      // Reset focus when the grid re-renders (search/filter changes) so the
      // highlight doesn't stick on a card that just left the viewport.
      var resetObserver = new MutationObserver(function(){ idx = -1; clearFocus(); });
      var gridContainer = document.getElementById('lib');
      if (gridContainer) resetObserver.observe(gridContainer, { childList: true, subtree: false });
    })();
    </script>
    <style>
      /* Gold focus ring + left-bar on j/k-navigated cards. Uses outline (not
         border) so it doesn't shift layout, and the box-shadow doubles as
         a subtle glow against the dark background. */
      .hub-card.kn-card-focus,
      .entry.kn-card-focus {
        outline: 2px solid #FFD740 !important;
        outline-offset: 2px;
        box-shadow: 0 0 0 1px rgba(255,215,64,0.2), 0 0 18px rgba(255,215,64,0.35) !important;
        position: relative;
      }
      .entry.kn-card-focus::before {
        content: "";
        position: absolute;
        left: -10px;
        top: 6px;
        bottom: 6px;
        width: 4px;
        background: linear-gradient(180deg, #FFD740, #E8710A);
        border-radius: 2px;
        box-shadow: 0 0 8px rgba(255,215,64,0.6);
      }
    </style>
    <div class="tag-chips" id="tag-chips"></div>
    <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center;margin-top:8px;">
      <button id="starred-filter" type="button" style="display:none;background:rgba(255,215,64,0.12);border:1px solid rgba(255,215,64,0.45);border-radius:99px;color:#FFD740;font-family:inherit;font-size:0.74rem;font-weight:800;letter-spacing:0.08em;padding:6px 14px;cursor:pointer;text-transform:uppercase;">★ Starred · <span id="starred-count">0</span></button>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- Recently viewed strip (t43) — horizontal pill row of the last 6
       entries this visitor opened. Source: localStorage 'pulse_recent_v1'
       (pushed by pulse-machine-entry.js on every entry-page view). Hidden
       when the list is empty (first-time visitors see nothing — no shift).
       Pairs with the NEW badge (t29) so return visitors get TWO orientation
       cues: what's new + where I was. -->
  <div id="kn-recent-strip" style="display:none;max-width:1100px;margin:18px auto 6px;padding:0 14px;">
    <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;margin-bottom:6px;">
      <span style="font-size:0.56rem;font-weight:900;letter-spacing:0.22em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#FFD740;">↻ Recently viewed</span>
      <button id="kn-recent-clear" type="button" title="Clear recent" style="margin-left:auto;background:transparent;border:none;color:rgba(237,229,216,0.45);font-size:0.6rem;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:0.08em;text-transform:uppercase;cursor:pointer;font-family:inherit;padding:2px 6px;">Clear</button>
    </div>
    <div id="kn-recent-pills" style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px;"></div>
  </div>
  <style>
    .kn-recent-pill {
      display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 6px;
      max-width: 320px;
      padding: 5px 11px;
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(232,113,10,0.14), rgba(255,140,26,0.06));
      border: 1px solid rgba(232,113,10,0.32);

    .kn-recent-pill:hover {
