  <script>
  // Render the Recently-viewed strip from localStorage. Pure read; no
  // dependency on the entry list having loaded yet. Re-renders on storage
  // events too so opening a new tab + viewing an entry updates this tab.
  (function(){
    var KEY = 'pulse_recent_v1';
    var strip = document.getElementById('kn-recent-strip');
    var pills = document.getElementById('kn-recent-pills');
    var clearBtn = document.getElementById('kn-recent-clear');
    if (!strip || !pills) return;
    function esc(s){ return String(s||'').replace(/[&<>"']/g, function(c){
      return { '&':'&amp;', '<':'&lt;', '>':'&gt;', '"':'&quot;', "'":'&#39;' }[c];
    }); }
    function render() {
      var raw; try { raw = localStorage.getItem(KEY); } catch(e){ strip.style.display='none'; return; }
      if (!raw) { strip.style.display='none'; return; }
      var list; try { list = JSON.parse(raw); } catch(e){ strip.style.display='none'; return; }
      if (!Array.isArray(list) || !list.length) { strip.style.display='none'; return; }
      var top = list.slice(0, 6);
      pills.innerHTML = top.map(function(r){
        if (!r || !r.id) return '';
        var url = r.url || (/^st\d+$/i.test(r.id) ? '/sales-trainings/' : '/knowledge/') + r.id;
        var qShort = (r.question || r.id).slice(0, 60);
        return '<a class="kn-recent-pill" href="' + esc(url) + '" title="' + esc(r.question||'') + '">'
             + '<span class="kn-recent-id">' + esc(r.id) + '</span>'
             + '<span class="kn-recent-q">' + esc(qShort) + (r.question && r.question.length > 60 ? '…' : '') + '</span>'
             + '</a>';
      }).join('');
      strip.style.display = 'block';
    }
    clearBtn.addEventListener('click', function(){
      try { localStorage.removeItem(KEY); } catch(e){}
      strip.style.display='none';
    });
    window.addEventListener('storage', function(e){ if (e.key === KEY) render(); });
    render();
  })();
  </script>

  <!-- t160 — Bold animated "THE KNOWLEDGE GRID" section-header banner. A
       striking full-width command divider that sits directly above the card
       grid, announcing where the library proper begins. Purely decorative +
       additive — no JS, no network, no layout coupling. Respects
       prefers-reduced-motion; hidden on print. -->
  <div id="kn-grid-header" aria-hidden="true">
    <span class="kgh-rule"></span>
    <span class="kgh-core">
      <span class="kgh-chevrons">»»»</span>
      <span class="kgh-title">THE&nbsp;KNOWLEDGE&nbsp;GRID</span>
      <span class="kgh-sub">live · cited · compounding</span>
    </span>
    <span class="kgh-rule"></span>
  </div>

  <!-- kn-grid-native-links v5 2026-05-24 -->
  <div class="lib" id="lib"></div>

  <div class="footer-note">
    Researched autonomously by Pulse Machine · Claude Sonnet 4.6 + live web search · Updated hourly · <a href="/privacy" style="color:rgba(255,140,26,0.7);">Privacy</a>
  </div>

  <script>
  (function(){
    'use strict';
    var libEl       = document.getElementById('lib');
    var statTotal   = document.getElementById('stat-total');
    var statToday   = document.getElementById('stat-today');
    var statTags    = document.getElementById('stat-tags');
    var tagChipsEl  = document.getElementById('tag-chips');
    var searchEl    = document.getElementById('q-search');

    var entries     = [];
    var readCounts  = {};   // { qNNNN: 7-day-sum } — populated by loadReadCounts()
    var viewTotals  = {};   // { qNNNN: lifetime total } — from entry-view ?all=1 totals
    var top10Weekly = null; // { items, updated_at } — hourly snapshot from entry-view ?top10=1
    var activeTag   = null;
    var showStarredOnly = false;
    function getStars() {
      try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('pulse-stars-v1') || '[]') || []; } catch(e){ return []; }
    }
    function refreshStarPill() {
      var btn = document.getElementById('starred-filter');
      var cnt = document.getElementById('starred-count');
      var stars = getStars();
      if (btn) btn.style.display = stars.length ? 'inline-block' : 'none';
      if (cnt) cnt.textContent = stars.length;
      if (btn) {
        btn.style.background = showStarredOnly ? 'rgba(255,215,64,0.30)' : 'rgba(255,215,64,0.12)';
        btn.style.color = showStarredOnly ? '#000' : '#FFD740';
      }
    }
    refreshStarPill();
    setInterval(refreshStarPill, 2000);
    document.addEventListener('click', function(e){
      if (e.target && e.target.id === 'starred-filter') {
        showStarredOnly = !showStarredOnly;
        refreshStarPill();
        if (typeof paint === 'function') paint();
      }
    });
    var loadedFull  = {};   // id → full entry (lazy-loaded)
    (function(){
      var pin = '';
      try {
        pin = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get('pin') || '';
        if (!pin) {
          var raw = sessionStorage.getItem('pulse_pin_entry');
          if (raw) pin = (JSON.parse(raw) || {}).id || '';
        }
      } catch (_e) {}
      if (pin) window.__pinEntryId = String(pin).replace(/[^\w-]/g, '');
    })();
    var renderLimit = 30;   // legacy — initial card cap (kept for any old callers)
    var PAGE_SIZE   = 30;
    var currentPage = 1;    // 1-indexed; reset to 1 on filter / search / tag change

    function escTxt(s) {
      return String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function(c){
        return { '&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;' }[c];
      });
    }
    function searchTokens(q) {
      return String(q || '').toLowerCase().trim().split(/\s+/).filter(function(t){ return t.length > 0; });
    }
    function entrySearchHay(e) {
      return ((e.question || '') + ' ' + (e.tags || []).join(' ') + ' ' + (e.id || '')).toLowerCase();
    }
    function entryMatchesSearch(e, search) {
      if (!search) return true;
      var tokens = searchTokens(search);
      if (!tokens.length) return true;
      var hay = entrySearchHay(e);
      for (var i = 0; i < tokens.length; i++) {
        if (hay.indexOf(tokens[i]) === -1) return false;
      }
      return true;
    }
    function highlightQuestionText(text, search) {
      if (!search) return escTxt(text);
      var html = escTxt(text);
      var toks = searchTokens(search).slice().sort(function(a, b){ return b.length - a.length; });
      for (var i = 0; i < toks.length; i++) {
        var safe = toks[i].replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&');
        if (!safe) continue;
        html = html.replace(new RegExp('(' + safe + ')', 'gi'), '<mark class="q-hit">$1</mark>');
      }
      return html;
    }
    function hostOf(u) { try { return new URL(u).hostname.replace(/^www\./,''); } catch(e) { return u; } }

    function todayMs() { var d = new Date(); d.setHours(0,0,0,0); return d.getTime(); }

    function isVisitorQueueEntry(e) {
      if (!e) return false;
      return !!e.pending || e.source === 'visitor' || /^vq_/i.test(String(e.id || ''));
    }
    function entryQNum(e) {
      var m = String(e && e.id || '').match(/^q(\d+)$/i);
      return m ? parseInt(m[1], 10) : 0;
    }

    function paint() {
      var todayStart = todayMs();
      var todayCount = entries.filter(function(e){ return e.ts >= todayStart; }).length;
      var tagSet = {};
      entries.forEach(function(e){ (e.tags || []).forEach(function(t){ tagSet[t] = (tagSet[t]||0)+1; }); });

      // stat-total is owned by the status-endpoint refresh below. Don't overwrite
      // it here with the filtered entry count or the number flickers on paint.
      statToday.textContent = todayCount;
      statTags.textContent  = Object.keys(tagSet).length;

      if (typeof window.paint !== 'function') window.paint = paint;

      // Top 12 tags by frequency
      var topTags = Object.keys(tagSet).sort(function(a,b){ return tagSet[b]-tagSet[a]; }).slice(0,12);
      tagChipsEl.innerHTML = topTags.map(function(t){
        var cls = (t === activeTag) ? ' active' : '';
        return '<button class="tag-chip'+cls+'" data-tag="'+escTxt(t)+'">'+escTxt(t)+' ('+tagSet[t]+')</button>';
      }).join('');

      var search = (searchEl.value || '').toLowerCase().trim();
      var starredSet = showStarredOnly ? new Set(getStars()) : null;
      // t108 — min-qs filter from number-key shortcuts (1=≥6, 2=≥7, 3=≥8,
      // 4=≥9, 5=qs=10). 0 = off. Layers on top of the other filters.
      var minQs = parseInt(window.__minQs, 10);
      if (!(minQs >= 6 && minQs <= 10)) minQs = 0;
      var filtered = entries.filter(function(e){
        if (starredSet && !starredSet.has(e.id)) return false;
        if (minQs && (typeof e.quality_score !== 'number' ? 5 : e.quality_score) < minQs) return false;
        if (activeTag && !(e.tags || []).includes(activeTag)) return false;
        if (search && !entryMatchesSearch(e, search)) return false;
        return true;
      });
      // Live result-count line under the search input. Only shown when the
      // filter is actively narrowing the set — empty search hides the line
      // so the layout doesn't shift on every keystroke.
      var resultCountEl = document.getElementById('q-result-count');
      if (resultCountEl) {
        // t90 — always show the count, not only when a filter is active.
        // Visitors landing cold deserve to see "1,740 entries" up front; with
        // a filter applied we keep the existing "N of M · matching X" line.
        if (entries.length > 0) {
          var hasFilter = !!(search || activeTag || starredSet || minQs);
          var html;
          if (hasFilter) {
            var parts = [];
            if (search)     parts.push('matching "' + escTxt(search) + '"');
            if (activeTag)  parts.push('tagged ' + escTxt(activeTag));
            if (starredSet) parts.push('starred');
            if (minQs)      parts.push('qs ' + (minQs === 10 ? '= 10' : '≥ ' + minQs));
            html = '<span style="color:#FFD740;">' + filtered.length.toLocaleString() + '</span>'
                 + ' of ' + entries.length.toLocaleString() + ' entries · ' + parts.join(' · ');
            if (search && filtered.length > PAGE_SIZE) {
              html += ' · scroll the list for all matches';
            }
          } else {
            html = '<span style="color:rgba(237,229,216,0.55);">'
                 + entries.length.toLocaleString() + ' entries</span>'
                 + ' &middot; <span style="opacity:.55;">press <kbd style="background:#0c0d10;border:1px solid rgba(232,113,10,0.35);border-radius:4px;padding:0 5px;font:600 0.62rem ui-monospace,Menlo,Consolas,monospace;color:#FFB04A;">?</kbd> for shortcuts</span>';
          }
          resultCountEl.innerHTML = html;
          resultCountEl.style.display = 'block';
        } else {
          resultCountEl.style.display = 'none';
        }
      }
      // Published answers first (newest at top of page 1). Visitor/pending
      // cards sink to the bottom — 700+ pending rows were burying economy posts.
      filtered.sort(function(a, b){
        var pin = window.__pinEntryId;
        if (pin) {
          if (a.id === pin) return -1;
          if (b.id === pin) return 1;
        }
        var aVis = isVisitorQueueEntry(a);
        var bVis = isVisitorQueueEntry(b);
        if (aVis !== bVis) return aVis ? 1 : -1;
        var tsDiff = (b.ts || 0) - (a.ts || 0);
        if (tsDiff !== 0) return tsDiff;
        return entryQNum(b) - entryQNum(a);
      });

      if (!filtered.length) {
        if (!entries.length) {
          libEl.innerHTML = '<div class="empty"><b>The library is just waking up.</b><br><br>The Machine writes a new researched answer every hour. Come back in a day or two — by then there will be 24-50 entries to browse.<br><br>Or <a href="/themachine">ask The Machine directly</a> right now and your question becomes one of the next entries.</div>';
        } else {
          libEl.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">No entries match this filter.</div>';
        }
        return;
      }

      // Keyword search: show every match in one scrollable grid (no 30-per-page cap).
      var showAllMatches = !!search;
      if (showAllMatches) currentPage = 1;
      libEl.classList.toggle('lib-search-all', showAllMatches);
      libEl.classList.toggle('hub-grid', !showAllMatches);

      function hubTagsLine(tags) {
        if (!Array.isArray(tags) || !tags.length) return '';
        var skip = { 'economy-mode': 1, 'revops-google': 1, 'revops-100': 1, 'revops': 1 };
        return tags.filter(function(t){ return t && !skip[t]; }).slice(0, 3)
          .map(function(t){ return '<span class="tag">' + escTxt(t) + '</span>'; }).join('');
      }

      // Pagination — slice by current page unless searching (then show all matches).
      var totalPages = showAllMatches
        ? 1
        : Math.max(1, Math.ceil(filtered.length / PAGE_SIZE));
      if (currentPage > totalPages) currentPage = totalPages;
      if (currentPage < 1) currentPage = 1;
      // t132 — expose the post-clamp page geometry so the keyboard page-nav
      // handlers ([ ] / Home / End) can read totalPages outside paint() scope
      // and surface a "Page N / M" toast. Refreshed on every paint().
      window.__libTotalPages = totalPages;
      window.__libCurrentPage = currentPage;
      var pageStart = (currentPage - 1) * PAGE_SIZE;
      var visible = showAllMatches
        ? filtered
        : filtered.slice(pageStart, pageStart + PAGE_SIZE);

      var cardsHtml = visible.map(function(e){
        // Tag pills are clickable links to per-tag landing pages — multiplies
        // internal-link signal for SEO and lets visitors jump to a curated
        // cluster instead of just filtering the current page.
        // Tags removed 2026-05-13 per owner — hyper-specific tags weren't useful as navigation.
        var tagsHtml = '';
        var dateObj = new Date(e.ts);
        var date = dateObj.toLocaleDateString(undefined, {month:'short', day:'numeric', year:'numeric'})
                 + ' · '
                 + dateObj.toLocaleTimeString(undefined, {hour:'2-digit', minute:'2-digit'});
        // Indexed indicator — entries older than 14 days have moved past the
        // Google crawl window and are likely fully indexed. Small green mark
        // distinguishes "in queue" from "indexed."
        var ageMs = Date.now() - (e.ts || 0);
        var isIndexed = ageMs > 14 * 86400000;
        var indexedIcon = isIndexed
          ? '<span title="Likely indexed by Google (>14 days since publish)" style="display:inline-block;color:#39FF14;font-size:0.85em;font-weight:900;margin-right:5px;vertical-align:middle;text-shadow:0 0 4px rgba(57,255,20,0.6);">✓</span>'
          : '<span title="Pending Google index (within 14-day window)" style="display:inline-block;color:rgba(255,180,90,0.55);font-size:0.85em;font-weight:700;margin-right:5px;vertical-align:middle;">◌</span>';
        // Highlight all "How'd you fix [Company]..." revenue-fix entries
        var isFeatured = /^how'?d you fix /i.test(e.question || '')
          || (e.tags || []).indexOf('cro-candidate-pitch') !== -1
          || (e.tags || []).indexOf('executive-outreach') !== -1
          || (e.tags || []).indexOf('revenue-fix') !== -1;
        var featuredBadge = isFeatured
          ? '<span style="display:inline-block;background:linear-gradient(135deg,rgba(232,113,10,0.9),rgba(184,80,30,0.75));color:#fff;padding:3px 10px;border-radius:99px;font-size:0.55rem;font-weight:800;letter-spacing:0.14em;text-transform:uppercase;margin-right:8px;vertical-align:middle;">★ Featured</span>'
          : '';
        var isPending = !!e.pending;
        // Green "I" marker — solid once IndexNow has confirmed the ping,
        // flashing while still queued (not yet indexed). Replaces the
        // verbose "⊙ Indexed" pill so the badge row stays compact.
        var indexedPill = e.was_indexed_at
          ? '<span title="Indexed — IndexNow ping confirmed" style="display:inline-block;color:#39FF14;font-family:Georgia,\'Times New Roman\',serif;font-style:italic;font-size:1rem;font-weight:900;line-height:1;margin-right:8px;padding:0 2px;vertical-align:middle;text-shadow:0 0 6px rgba(57,255,20,0.85),0 0 2px rgba(57,255,20,0.6);">I</span>'
          : '<span title="Currently indexing — awaiting IndexNow confirmation" style="display:inline-block;color:#39FF14;font-family:Georgia,\'Times New Roman\',serif;font-style:italic;font-size:1rem;font-weight:900;line-height:1;margin-right:8px;padding:0 2px;vertical-align:middle;text-shadow:0 0 6px rgba(57,255,20,0.85),0 0 2px rgba(57,255,20,0.6);animation:pulsePolish 1.4s ease-in-out infinite;">I</span>';
        // Gold "S" — SEO Optimized: 10/10 verified AND indexed.
        // Cyan "F" — Fresh: 10/10 polished within last 30 days.
        // F (Fresh) and S (SEO Optimized) badges removed 2026-05-12 per owner —
        // visual noise on 10/10 cards. Only the I (Indexed) badge stays.
        var isSeoOptimized = false;
        var isFresh = false;
        var seoBadge = isSeoOptimized
          ? '<span title="SEO Optimized — 10/10 verified + indexed" style="display:inline-block;color:#FFD740;font-family:Georgia,\'Times New Roman\',serif;font-style:italic;font-size:1rem;font-weight:900;line-height:1;margin-right:8px;padding:0 2px;vertical-align:middle;text-shadow:0 0 6px rgba(255,215,64,0.85),0 0 2px rgba(255,215,64,0.6);">S</span>'
          : '';
        var freshBadge = isFresh
          ? '<span title="Fresh — polished within last 30 days" style="display:inline-block;color:#39E5FF;font-family:Georgia,\'Times New Roman\',serif;font-style:italic;font-size:1rem;font-weight:900;line-height:1;margin-right:8px;padding:0 2px;vertical-align:middle;text-shadow:0 0 6px rgba(57,229,255,0.85),0 0 2px rgba(57,229,255,0.6);">F</span>'
          : '';
        // Reads badge — 7-day count from entry-view function. Hot (>=5) is bright,
        // warm (1-4) is dimmer, cold (0) is muted gray. Updates every 60s.
        var rc = readCounts[e.id] || 0;
        var vt = viewTotals[e.id] || 0;
        var viewsBadge = vt > 0
          ? '<span title="'+vt.toLocaleString()+' total views" style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:3px;background:rgba(57,255,20,0.10);border:1px solid rgba(57,255,20,0.35);color:#9ef0a8;padding:1px 8px;border-radius:99px;font-size:0.6rem;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:0.06em;margin-right:6px;">👁 '+vt.toLocaleString()+'</span>'
          : '';
        var readsBadge = rc >= 5
          ? '<span title="'+rc+' reads in last 7 days" style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:3px;background:rgba(255,215,64,0.14);border:1px solid rgba(255,215,64,0.45);color:#FFD740;padding:1px 8px;border-radius:99px;font-size:0.6rem;font-weight:800;letter-spacing:0.08em;margin-right:6px;">🔥 '+rc+'</span>'
          : (rc > 0
            ? '<span title="'+rc+' reads in last 7 days" style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:3px;background:rgba(232,113,10,0.10);border:1px solid rgba(232,113,10,0.35);color:rgba(255,140,26,0.85);padding:1px 8px;border-radius:99px;font-size:0.6rem;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:0.08em;margin-right:6px;">🔥 '+rc+'</span>'
            : '<span title="0 reads in last 7 days" style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:3px;color:rgba(237,229,216,0.32);padding:1px 6px;font-size:0.58rem;font-weight:600;letter-spacing:0.08em;margin-right:6px;">○ 0</span>');
        var isNew = ageMs < 24 * 3600 * 1000;
        var newBadge = isNew
          ? '<span title="Published in the last 24h" style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:5px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,rgba(255,215,64,0.95),rgba(232,159,10,0.85));color:#1a1208;padding:3px 10px;border-radius:99px;font-size:0.55rem;font-weight:900;letter-spacing:0.16em;text-transform:uppercase;margin-right:8px;vertical-align:middle;box-shadow:0 0 12px rgba(255,215,64,0.55);"><span style="width:6px;height:6px;border-radius:50%;background:#fff;box-shadow:0 0 6px #fff;animation:pulsePolish 1.4s ease-in-out infinite;"></span>New</span>'
          : '';
        var cardBadges = newBadge + indexedPill + featuredBadge;
        // Highlight search matches in the visible question text. Uses a
        // case-insensitive substring match — same semantics as the filter
        // above, so every visible card reads as "matched". `<mark class="q-hit">`
        // gets styled via the inline <style> block next to #q-result-count.
        var questionHtml = highlightQuestionText(e.question, search);
        var CARD_STYLE = showAllMatches
          ? ' style="border:2px solid rgba(255,140,26,0.42); background:linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(255,140,26,0.055), rgba(10,14,20,0.02) 60%); box-shadow:0 0 12px rgba(255,140,26,0.14);"'
          : '';
        if (!showAllMatches) {
          if (isPending) {
            return '<div class="hub-card visitor-pending" data-id="'+e.id+'">'
              + '<div class="hub-meta-row"><span class="hub-pill visitor">Writing now</span><span>'+escTxt(e.id)+'</span></div>'
              + '<h3 class="hub-card-title">'+questionHtml+'</h3>'
              + '<div class="hub-tags"><span class="tag">visitor-asked</span></div>'
              + '<div class="hub-footer"><span>Researching — refresh soon</span><span class="open">Pending</span></div>'
              + '</div>';
          }
          return '<a class="hub-card" href="/knowledge/'+escTxt(e.id)+'" data-id="'+e.id+'">'
            + '<div class="hub-meta-row"><span class="hub-pill">RevOps answer</span><span>'+escTxt(e.id)+'</span></div>'
            + '<h3 class="hub-card-title">'+questionHtml+'</h3>'
            + '<div class="hub-tags">'+hubTagsLine(e.tags)+'</div>'
            + '<div class="hub-footer"><span>Pulse · sourced operator answer</span><span class="open">Open answer</span></div>'
            + '</a>';
        }
        // Search-all mode: compact list rows (legacy layout)
        if (isPending) {
          return '<div class="entry visitor-pending" data-id="'+e.id+'"'+CARD_STYLE+'>'
            + '<div class="entry-question">'+questionHtml+'</div>'
            + '<div class="entry-badges">'+cardBadges+'</div>'
            + '<div class="entry-meta"><span style="color:rgba(255,180,90,0.9);font-weight:800;">✍ A visitor question — researching now. Refresh in a few minutes.</span></div>'
            + '</div>';
        }
        return '<a class="entry entry-link" href="/knowledge/'+escTxt(e.id)+'" data-id="'+e.id+'"'+CARD_STYLE+'>'
          + '<div class="entry-question">'+questionHtml+'</div>'
          + '<div class="entry-badges">'+cardBadges+'</div>'
          + '<div class="entry-meta">'+viewsBadge+readsBadge+tagsHtml+'<span>'+indexedIcon+date+'</span>'
          + '<span class="entry-cta" aria-hidden="true">Read answer →</span>'
          + '<span role="button" tabindex="0" class="copy-id-btn" data-copy-id="'+e.id+'" title="Copy '+e.id+' to clipboard" aria-label="Copy entry ID '+e.id+'"><span aria-hidden="true">⧉</span>'+e.id+'</span>'
          + '</div>'
          + '</a>';
      }).join('');

      // Pagination bar — numbered pages with prev/next + first/last shortcuts.
      // Shows: « ‹ 1 ... (cur-2) (cur-1) [cur] (cur+1) (cur+2) ... N › »
      function buildPagerHtml(){
        if (totalPages <= 1) return '';
        var btn = function(label, page, opts){
          opts = opts || {};
          var disabled = !!opts.disabled;
          var active = !!opts.active;
          var bg = active
            ? 'background:linear-gradient(135deg,#FF8C1A,#E8710A);color:#0a0a0a;border:1px solid #FF8C1A;'
            : 'background:rgba(232,113,10,0.10);color:#FFD7A8;border:1px solid rgba(232,113,10,0.35);';
          var dim = disabled ? 'opacity:0.35;cursor:not-allowed;' : 'cursor:pointer;';
          var fw = active ? '900' : '700';
          return '<button class="pager-btn" data-page="' + page + '"' + (disabled ? ' disabled' : '') +
            ' style="' + bg + dim + 'min-width:38px;padding:8px 12px;border-radius:7px;font-family:inherit;font-size:0.78rem;font-weight:' + fw + ';letter-spacing:0.06em;margin:3px;">' +
            label + '</button>';
        };
        // Build the visible page-number set — current ± 2, plus first & last.
        var pages = new Set([1, totalPages]);
        for (var i = currentPage - 2; i <= currentPage + 2; i++) {
          if (i >= 1 && i <= totalPages) pages.add(i);
        }
        var sortedPages = Array.from(pages).sort(function(a,b){ return a - b; });
        var pagesHtml = '';
        var prev = 0;
        sortedPages.forEach(function(p){
          if (prev && p > prev + 1) {
            pagesHtml += '<span style="display:inline-block;min-width:28px;text-align:center;color:rgba(237,229,216,0.4);font-size:0.78rem;margin:0 2px;">…</span>';
          }
          pagesHtml += btn(String(p), p, { active: p === currentPage });
          prev = p;
        });
        return '<nav aria-label="Library pagination" style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;justify-content:center;align-items:center;gap:0;margin:28px 0 12px;padding-top:18px;border-top:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.06);">' +
          '<span style="color:rgba(237,229,216,0.55);font-size:0.68rem;letter-spacing:0.16em;text-transform:uppercase;margin-right:12px;">Page ' + currentPage + ' / ' + totalPages + '</span>' +
          btn('« First', 1, { disabled: currentPage === 1 }) +
          btn('‹ Prev', Math.max(1, currentPage - 1), { disabled: currentPage === 1 }) +
          pagesHtml +
          btn('Next ›', Math.min(totalPages, currentPage + 1), { disabled: currentPage === totalPages }) +
          btn('Last »', totalPages, { disabled: currentPage === totalPages }) +
        '</nav>';
      }
      var pagerHtml = buildPagerHtml();

      var searchBannerHtml = '';
      if (showAllMatches && filtered.length > PAGE_SIZE) {
        searchBannerHtml = '<div class="kn-search-scroll-hint" role="status">Showing all '
          + filtered.length.toLocaleString() + ' matches — scroll to browse</div>';
      }

      libEl.innerHTML = searchBannerHtml + cardsHtml + pagerHtml;
    }

    // t166 — Render the "🔥 Top 10 Most Viewed This Week" widget. Pure reuse of
    // in-memory data: `readCounts` is the genuine last-7-days per-entry read
    // sum the page already fetched from entry-view ?all=1 (the same data that
    // drives the per-card 🔥 badge), and `entries` supplies the question text.
    // No new network request. Called after entries OR readCounts load/refresh.
    // The widget stays hidden until there is at least one entry with reads.
    function top10RowsHtml(ranked) {
      return ranked.map(function(r, i){
        var q = escTxt(r.question || (r.entry && r.entry.question) || r.id);
        var n = r.count;
        var id = r.id;
        return '<a class="kn-t10-row" href="/knowledge/' + escTxt(id) + '" '
          + 'title="' + q + ' — ' + n + ' read' + (n === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' in the last 7 days">'
          + '<span class="kn-t10-rank" aria-hidden="true">' + (i + 1) + '</span>'
          + '<span class="kn-t10-q">' + q + '</span>'
          + '<span class="kn-t10-count">🔥 ' + n + ' <span class="kn-t10-views">read' + (n === 1 ? '' : 's') + '</span></span>'
          + '</a>';
      }).join('');
    }

    function paintTop10Sub(updatedAt) {
      var sub = document.getElementById('kn-t10-sub');
      if (!sub) return;
      if (!updatedAt) {
        sub.textContent = 'last 7 days · updates hourly';
        return;
      }
      var d = new Date(updatedAt);
      sub.textContent = 'last 7 days · updated ' + d.toLocaleString(undefined, { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit' });
    }

    function renderTop10() {
      var box = document.getElementById('kn-top10');
      var list = document.getElementById('kn-top10-list');
      if (!box || !list) return;
      if (top10Weekly && top10Weekly.items && top10Weekly.items.length) {
        box.hidden = false;
        list.innerHTML = top10RowsHtml(top10Weekly.items);
        paintTop10Sub(top10Weekly.updated_at);
        return;
      }
      var byId = {};
      entries.forEach(function(e){ if (e && e.id) byId[e.id] = e; });
      var ranked = Object.keys(readCounts || {})
        .map(function(id){ return { id: id, count: readCounts[id] || 0, entry: byId[id] }; })
        .filter(function(r){ return r.count > 0 && r.entry && r.entry.question; })
        .sort(function(a, b){ return b.count - a.count; })
        .slice(0, 10);
      if (!ranked.length) { box.hidden = true; return; }
      box.hidden = false;
      list.innerHTML = top10RowsHtml(ranked);
      paintTop10Sub(null);
    }

    function loadTop10Weekly() {
      return fetch('/.netlify/functions/entry-view?top10=1', { cache: 'no-store' })
        .then(function(r){ return r.ok ? r.json() : null; })
        .then(function(d){
          if (d && d.ok && Array.isArray(d.items) && d.items.length) {
            top10Weekly = d;
            renderTop10();
          }
        })
        .catch(function(){ /* fallback: renderTop10 from readCounts */ });
    }

    // Markdown-ish renderer matching pulse-new.html
    var YT_RE = /^https?:\/\/(?:www\.|m\.)?(?:youtube\.com\/(?:watch\?[^ ]*v=|embed\/|shorts\/)|youtu\.be\/)([A-Za-z0-9_-]{6,})/;
    var IMG_RE = /\.(?:png|jpe?g|gif|webp|svg)(?:\?.*)?$/i;
    var URL_RE = /\bhttps?:\/\/[^\s<>"'\)\]]+/gi;
    function youtubeId(u) { var m = u.match(YT_RE); return m ? m[1] : null; }
    function renderInline(text) {
      // Stash markdown links behind opaque placeholders so the bare-URL pass
      // can't re-match URLs inside their own href attributes (the bug that
      // produced HTML-soup like `[label](url" target="_blank">label)` —
      // markdown link gets HTML-ified, then URL_RE re-wraps the inner URL
      // and breaks the outer tag).
      var mdLinks = [];
      text = text.replace(/\[([^\]]+)\]\((https?:\/\/[^\s)]+)\)/g, function(_, label, url){
        var i = mdLinks.length;
        mdLinks.push('<a href="'+url+'" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">'+escTxt(label)+'</a>');
        return ' MDLINK' + i + ' ';
      });
      text = text.replace(/`([^`]+)`/g, function(_, code){ return '<code>'+escTxt(code)+'</code>'; });
      text = text.replace(/\*\*([^*]+)\*\*/g, '<strong>$1</strong>');
      text = text.replace(URL_RE, function(u){ return '<a href="'+u+'" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">'+u+'</a>'; });
      text = text.replace(/ MDLINK(\d+) /g, function(_, i){ return mdLinks[parseInt(i, 10)]; });
      return text;
    }
    function renderTable(lines) {
      var rows = lines.map(function(l){ return l.replace(/^\||\|$/g,'').split('|').map(function(c){ return c.trim(); }); })
        .filter(function(r){ return !r.every(function(c){ return /^[:\-\s]+$/.test(c); }); });
      if (!rows.length) return '';
      var head = rows[0], body = rows.slice(1);
      return '<table><thead><tr>' + head.map(function(c){ return '<th>'+renderInline(escTxt(c))+'</th>'; }).join('') + '</tr></thead><tbody>'
        + body.map(function(r){ return '<tr>' + r.map(function(c){ return '<td>'+renderInline(escTxt(c))+'</td>'; }).join('') + '</tr>'; }).join('')
        + '</tbody></table>';
    }
    var _merSeq = 0;
    function renderMd(text) {
      text = String(text || '').replace(/\r\n/g,'\n').trim();
      var lines = text.split('\n'); var out = []; var i = 0;
      var paraBuf = [];
      function flush(){ if (!paraBuf.length) return; var j = paraBuf.join(' ').trim(); if (j) out.push('<p>'+renderInline(escTxt(j))+'</p>'); paraBuf=[]; }
      while (i < lines.length) {
        var line = lines[i], t = line.trim();
        if (/^```mermaid\s*$/i.test(t)) {
          flush(); i++; var mer = [];
          while (i < lines.length && !/^```/.test(lines[i].trim())) { mer.push(lines[i]); i++; }
          i++;
          var src = mer.join('\n').trim();
          if (src) {
            var id = 'lmer-' + (++_merSeq) + '-' + Date.now().toString(36);
            out.push('<div class="mermaid-wrap"><div class="mermaid" id="'+id+'">'+escTxt(src)+'</div></div>');
          }
          continue;
        }
        if (!t) { flush(); i++; continue; }
        // Blockquote — consecutive "> " lines become one <blockquote>, content
        // recursively rendered so nested H3 / lists inside work (Bottom Line).
        if (/^>\s?/.test(t)) {
          flush();
          var qlines = [];
          while (i < lines.length && /^>\s?/.test(lines[i].trim())) {
            qlines.push(lines[i].trim().replace(/^>\s?/, ''));
            i++;
          }
          out.push('<blockquote>' + renderMd(qlines.join('\n')) + '</blockquote>');
          continue;
        }
        if (/^---+\s*$/.test(t)) { flush(); out.push('<hr>'); i++; continue; }
        if (/^#{2,3}\s+/.test(t)) {
          flush();
          var lvl = t.match(/^(#{2,3})/)[1].length;
          var c = t.replace(/^#{2,3}\s+/, '');
          out.push('<h'+lvl+'>'+renderInline(escTxt(c))+'</h'+lvl+'>'); i++; continue;
        }
        if (/^\|.*\|/.test(t) && i+1 < lines.length && /^\|[\s:|-]+\|/.test(lines[i+1].trim())) {
          flush(); var tbl = [];
          while (i < lines.length && /^\|.*\|/.test(lines[i].trim())) { tbl.push(lines[i].trim()); i++; }
          out.push(renderTable(tbl)); continue;
        }
        if (/^[-•*]\s+/.test(t)) {
          flush(); var items = [];
          while (i < lines.length && /^[-•*]\s+/.test(lines[i].trim())) { items.push(lines[i].trim().replace(/^[-•*]\s+/,'')); i++; }
          out.push('<ul>'+items.map(function(x){ return '<li>'+renderInline(escTxt(x))+'</li>'; }).join('')+'</ul>'); continue;
        }
        if (/^\d+\.\s+/.test(t)) {
          flush(); var nums = [];
          while (i < lines.length && /^\d+\.\s+/.test(lines[i].trim())) { nums.push(lines[i].trim().replace(/^\d+\.\s+/,'')); i++; }
          out.push('<ol>'+nums.map(function(x){ return '<li>'+renderInline(escTxt(x))+'</li>'; }).join('')+'</ol>'); continue;
        }
        paraBuf.push(t); i++;
      }
      flush();
      return out.join('');
    }

    function viewsBannerHtml(total) {
      var v = typeof total === 'number' ? total : 0;
      return '<div class="entry-views-banner" role="status" style="margin:0 0 16px;padding:10px 14px;background:rgba(57,255,20,0.08);border:1px solid rgba(57,255,20,0.32);border-radius:10px;color:#9ef0a8;font-size:0.78rem;font-weight:800;letter-spacing:0.06em;">👁 '
        + v.toLocaleString('en-US') + (v === 1 ? ' person viewed this answer' : ' people viewed this answer') + '</div>';
    }
    function recordEntryView(id, onTotal) {
      fetch('/.netlify/functions/entry-view?id=' + encodeURIComponent(id))
        .then(function(r){ return r.ok ? r.json() : null; })
        .then(function(j){
          var total = (j && typeof j.total === 'number') ? j.total : 0;
          viewTotals[id] = total;
          if (onTotal) onTotal(total);
          try {
            var sk = 'pulse-viewed-' + id;
            if (sessionStorage.getItem(sk)) return;
            sessionStorage.setItem(sk, '1');
            fetch('/.netlify/functions/entry-view', {
              method: 'POST',
              headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
              body: JSON.stringify({ id: id }),
              keepalive: true,
            }).then(function(r){ return r.json(); }).then(function(p){
              if (p && typeof p.total === 'number') {
                viewTotals[id] = p.total;
                if (onTotal) onTotal(p.total);
              }
            }).catch(function(){});
          } catch (e) {}
        })
        .catch(function(){ if (onTotal) onTotal(viewTotals[id] || 0); });
    }

    function expand(id) {
      var bodyEl = document.getElementById('body-' + id);
      if (!bodyEl) return;
      if (bodyEl.classList.contains('show')) { bodyEl.classList.remove('show'); return; }
      var viewBanner = '';
      recordEntryView(id, function(total){ viewBanner = viewsBannerHtml(total); });
      if (loadedFull[id]) {
        bodyEl.innerHTML = viewsBannerHtml(viewTotals[id] || 0) + loadedFull[id]._html;
        bodyEl.classList.add('show');
        runMermaid(bodyEl);
        recordEntryView(id, function(total){
          if (!bodyEl.classList.contains('show')) return;
          bodyEl.innerHTML = viewsBannerHtml(total) + loadedFull[id]._html;
          runMermaid(bodyEl);
        });
        return;
      }
      bodyEl.innerHTML = '<div style="color:rgba(237,229,216,0.5);">Loading…</div>';
      bodyEl.classList.add('show');
      fetch('/.netlify/functions/pulse-machine-library-list?id=' + encodeURIComponent(id))
        .then(function(r){ return r.ok ? r.json() : null; })
        .then(function(d){
          if (!d || !d.answer) { bodyEl.textContent = 'Could not load this entry.'; return; }
          // "In Layman's Terms?" — front and center, BEFORE the dense answer.
          var html = '<div class="layman-row" style="margin:0 0 18px;padding:14px 18px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,rgba(255,140,26,0.18),rgba(232,113,10,0.1));border:2px solid rgba(255,140,26,0.55);border-radius:12px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:12px;flex-wrap:wrap;box-shadow:0 3px 10px rgba(232,113,10,0.15);">'
            + '<div style="flex:1;min-width:180px;">'
            + '<div style="font-size:0.9rem;font-weight:900;color:#FFD7A8;line-height:1.3;margin-bottom:2px;">📖 Don\'t speak RevOps?</div>'
            + '<div style="font-size:0.75rem;color:rgba(237,229,216,0.7);line-height:1.4;">Get this in plain English — no jargon.</div>'
            + '</div>'
            + '<button class="layman-btn" type="button" data-layman-id="'+escTxt(id)+'" '
            + 'style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;background:linear-gradient(180deg,#FF8C1A,#E8710A);border:none;color:#0a0a0a;padding:12px 18px;border-radius:10px;font-family:inherit;font-size:0.82rem;font-weight:900;letter-spacing:0.04em;cursor:pointer;box-shadow:0 2px 8px rgba(232,113,10,0.4);white-space:nowrap;">'
            + '<span style="font-size:1.1rem;">🗣️</span><span class="lb-label">In Layman\'s Terms</span></button>'
            + '<div class="layman-panel" style="display:none;margin-top:14px;padding:16px 18px;background:rgba(255,140,26,0.05);border:1px dashed rgba(255,140,26,0.4);border-radius:10px;color:rgba(237,229,216,0.95);font-size:0.98rem;line-height:1.6;flex-basis:100%;">'
            + '<span style="font-size:0.6rem;font-weight:800;letter-spacing:0.22em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#FF8C1A;display:block;margin-bottom:8px;">Plain English</span>'
            + '<div class="lp-body"></div></div></div>';
          html += renderMd(d.answer);
          if (Array.isArray(d.sources) && d.sources.length) {
            html += '<div class="entry-sources"><div class="entry-sources-label">Sources cited</div>'
              + d.sources.map(function(s){
                  return '<a class="entry-source" href="'+s.url+'" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">'
                    + '<span class="es-host">'+escTxt(hostOf(s.url))+'</span>'
                    + '<span class="es-title">'+escTxt(s.title || s.url)+'</span></a>';
                }).join('')
              + '</div>';
          }
          // Deep Dive — internal cluster links to 3 related library entries.
          // Tells Google + AI search engines the library is an interconnected
          // knowledge graph, not a flat list. href points at the per-entry
          // page so right-click / middle-click / filtered-out entries still
          // navigate somewhere real instead of dying silently.
          var related = findRelated(d, entries, 3);
          if (related.length) {
            html += '<div class="entry-sources" style="margin-top:14px;"><div class="entry-sources-label">Deep dive · related in the library</div>'
              + related.map(function(r){
                  var tagPreview = (r.tags || []).slice(0, 2).join(' · ');
                  return '<a class="entry-source" href="/knowledge/'+r.id+'" data-deep-id="'+r.id+'">'
                    + '<span class="es-host">'+escTxt(tagPreview || '·')+'</span>'
                    + '<span class="es-title">'+escTxt(r.question)+'</span></a>';
                }).join('')
              + '</div>';
          }
          loadedFull[id] = { _html: html };
          bodyEl.innerHTML = viewBanner + html;
          runMermaid(bodyEl);
          recordEntryView(id, function(total){
            var banner = viewsBannerHtml(total);
            if (bodyEl.classList.contains('show')) {
              bodyEl.innerHTML = banner + html;
              runMermaid(bodyEl);
            }
          });
        })
        .catch(function(){ bodyEl.textContent = 'Could not load this entry.'; });
    }
    function runMermaid(scope) {
      if (window.mermaid && scope.querySelector('.mermaid')) {
        try { window.mermaid.run({ nodes: scope.querySelectorAll('.mermaid') }); } catch(e) {}
      }
    }

    libEl.addEventListener('click', function(e){
      // t112 — Copy q-ID to clipboard. Intercept BEFORE card-open routing so the
      // entry doesn't toggle when the chip is clicked. Uses navigator.clipboard
      // when available, falls back to a transient textarea + execCommand.
      var copyBtn = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.copy-id-btn');
      if (copyBtn) {
        e.stopPropagation();
        e.preventDefault();
        var qid = copyBtn.getAttribute('data-copy-id') || '';
        if (!qid) return;
        var doneFlash = function(){
          var orig = copyBtn.innerHTML;
          copyBtn.classList.add('copied');
          copyBtn.innerHTML = '<span aria-hidden="true">✓</span>copied';
          setTimeout(function(){
            copyBtn.classList.remove('copied');
            copyBtn.innerHTML = orig;
          }, 1100);
        };
        try {
          if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) {
            navigator.clipboard.writeText(qid).then(doneFlash, function(){
              // permission denied fallback
              var ta = document.createElement('textarea');
              ta.value = qid; ta.style.position = 'fixed'; ta.style.opacity = '0';
              document.body.appendChild(ta); ta.select();
              try { document.execCommand('copy'); doneFlash(); } catch(_){}
              document.body.removeChild(ta);
            });
          } else {
            var ta2 = document.createElement('textarea');
            ta2.value = qid; ta2.style.position = 'fixed'; ta2.style.opacity = '0';
            document.body.appendChild(ta2); ta2.select();
            try { document.execCommand('copy'); doneFlash(); } catch(_){}
            document.body.removeChild(ta2);
          }
        } catch(_) {}
        return;
      }
      // Pagination — jump to a specific page from the bottom pager.
      var pagerBtn = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.pager-btn');
      if (pagerBtn && !pagerBtn.disabled) {
        var p = parseInt(pagerBtn.getAttribute('data-page') || '1', 10);
        if (!isNaN(p) && p >= 1) {
          currentPage = p;
          paint();
          try { libEl.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' }); } catch(e2){}
        }
        return;
      }
      // Legacy load-more — kept for any old call sites; treats as "next page".
      if (e.target.id === 'load-more') {
        currentPage += 1;
        paint();
        return;
      }
      // Recycle button — reset paging to start, scroll to top of library
      if (e.target.id === 'recycle-library') {
        currentPage = 1;
        paint();
        try { libEl.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' }); } catch(e2){}
        return;
      }
      // "In Layman's Terms?" — translate the dense answer into plain English.
      var laymanBtn = e.target.closest('[data-layman-id]');
      if (laymanBtn) {
        e.stopPropagation();
        handleLayman(laymanBtn);
        return;
      }
      // Deep-dive cluster link — jump to a related entry inside the library.
      // If the target entry is in the current filtered DOM, scroll + expand.
      // If it's filtered out, fall through to the href so we navigate to
      // the per-entry page instead of dying silently on a missing DOM node.
      var deep = e.target.closest('[data-deep-id]');
      if (deep) {
        var targetId = deep.getAttribute('data-deep-id');
        var targetEl = document.querySelector('.entry[data-id="' + targetId + '"]');
        if (targetEl) {
          e.preventDefault();
          targetEl.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });
          setTimeout(function(){ expand(targetId); }, 350);
        }
        // else: let the browser follow href="/knowledge/<id>" to the per-entry page
        return;
      }
      var entry = e.target.closest('.entry');
      if (!entry || entry.classList.contains('visitor-pending')) return;
      if (entry.classList.contains('entry-link')) return;
      var eid = entry.getAttribute('data-id') || (entry.dataset && entry.dataset.id) || '';
      if (eid) window.location.href = '/knowledge/' + encodeURIComponent(eid);
    });

    function laymanMdToHtml(md) {
      var lines = (md || '').trim().split(/\n/);
      var html = '', inUl = false;
      function inline(s){
        return s.replace(/\*\*([^*]+)\*\*/g,'<strong>$1</strong>')
                .replace(/\*([^*]+)\*/g,'<em>$1</em>')
                .replace(/_([^_]+)_/g,'<em>$1</em>');
      }
      for (var i=0;i<lines.length;i++){
        var l = lines[i];
        if (/^\s*[-•]\s+/.test(l)) {
          if (!inUl){ html += '<ul style="margin:8px 0 10px 22px;padding:0;">'; inUl = true; }
          html += '<li>' + inline(escTxt(l.replace(/^\s*[-•]\s+/,''))) + '</li>';
        } else if (!l.trim()) {
          if (inUl){ html += '</ul>'; inUl = false; }
        } else {
          if (inUl){ html += '</ul>'; inUl = false; }
          html += '<p style="margin:0 0 10px;">' + inline(escTxt(l)) + '</p>';
        }
      }
      if (inUl) html += '</ul>';
      return html;
    }

    function handleLayman(btn) {
      var id = btn.getAttribute('data-layman-id');
      var panel = btn.parentElement.querySelector('.layman-panel');
      var body = panel.querySelector('.lp-body');
      var label = btn.querySelector('.lb-label');
      // Find the entry's expanded body and the dense markdown content within it
      // (everything in bodyEl except the layman-row container itself).
      var entryBody = btn.closest('.entry-body');
      var LABEL_LAYMAN = "In Layman's Terms";
      var LABEL_TECH = "Back to Technical Terms";
      function setDenseDisplay(disp) {
        if (!entryBody) return;
        var children = entryBody.children;
        for (var i = 0; i < children.length; i++) {
          if (!children[i].classList.contains('layman-row')) children[i].style.display = disp;
        }
      }
      function showLayman(text) {
        body.innerHTML = laymanMdToHtml(text);
        panel.style.display = 'block';
        setDenseDisplay('none');
        label.textContent = LABEL_TECH;
      }
      function showTechnical() {
        panel.style.display = 'none';
        setDenseDisplay('');
        label.textContent = LABEL_LAYMAN;
      }
      // Toggle off if currently showing
      if (panel.style.display === 'block') {
        showTechnical();
        return;
      }
      var cacheKey = 'layman:' + id;
      var cached = null;
      try { cached = sessionStorage.getItem(cacheKey); } catch(e){}
      if (cached) { showLayman(cached); return; }
      btn.disabled = true;
      label.textContent = 'Translating...';
      fetch('/.netlify/functions/laymanize', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ id: id }),
      }).then(function(r){ return r.json(); }).then(function(j){
        btn.disabled = false;
        if (j && j.ok && j.simplified) {
          try { sessionStorage.setItem(cacheKey, j.simplified); } catch(e){}
          showLayman(j.simplified);
        } else {
          label.textContent = LABEL_LAYMAN;
          body.innerHTML = '<p>Could not translate this one. Try again in a moment.</p>';
          panel.style.display = 'block';
        }
      }).catch(function(){
        btn.disabled = false;
        label.textContent = LABEL_LAYMAN;
        body.innerHTML = '<p>Network hiccup. Try again.</p>';
        panel.style.display = 'block';
      });
    }
    tagChipsEl.addEventListener('click', function(e){
      var c = e.target.closest('.tag-chip');
      if (!c) return;
      var t = c.dataset.tag;
      activeTag = (activeTag === t) ? null : t;
      currentPage = 1;  // reset to first page when filter changes
      paint();
    });
    searchEl.addEventListener('input', function(){
      currentPage = 1;  // reset to first page when search changes
      paint();
      acRefresh();      // t165 — keep the autocomplete dropdown in sync
    });

    // ── t165 — search-box autocomplete ────────────────────────────────────
    // Typeahead over entry TITLES, drawn from the in-memory `entries` array
    // already loaded — no network call. Up to AC_MAX quick-jump rows in the
    // dropdown; the main grid shows every text match when searching (paint()).
    // Multi-word queries require every token to match (question, tags, or id).
    // When more matches exist than AC_MAX, show a "+ N more" footer instead of
    // hiding the dropdown. Keyboard-navigable (up/down/enter).
    (function(){
      var acBox = document.getElementById('q-ac');
      if (!acBox || !searchEl) return;
      var AC_DROPDOWN_MAX = 80;
      var acMatches = [];   // current suggestion entries
      var acIndex = -1;     // keyboard-highlighted row, -1 = none

      function acHide(){
        acBox.classList.remove('is-open');
        acBox.innerHTML = '';
        acMatches = [];
        acIndex = -1;
        searchEl.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
        searchEl.removeAttribute('aria-activedescendant');
      }

      function acGo(entry){
        if (!entry || !entry.id) return;
        window.location.href = '/knowledge/' + encodeURIComponent(entry.id);
      }

      function acSetActive(i){
        var rows = acBox.querySelectorAll('.q-ac-item');
        for (var r = 0; r < rows.length; r++) rows[r].classList.remove('is-active');
        acIndex = i;
        if (i >= 0 && i < rows.length) {
          rows[i].classList.add('is-active');
          rows[i].scrollIntoView({ block: 'nearest' });
          searchEl.setAttribute('aria-activedescendant', rows[i].id);
        } else {
          searchEl.removeAttribute('aria-activedescendant');
        }
      }

      function acRefresh(){
        var q = (searchEl.value || '').toLowerCase().trim();
        if (!q) { acHide(); return; }
        acMatches = [];
        var acSeen = {};
        // t169 — entry-number search. If the query looks like an entry id
        // (bare digits, or a q/st prefix + digits, leading zeros optional),
        // match on e.id so typing "401", "q401", "q22", or "st28" pulls up
        // that answer directly. Exact id hits are listed first and row 0 is
        // pre-highlighted so Enter jumps straight to the answer. Bare digits
        // match both q#### and st#### ids.
        var idm = q.match(/^(q|st)?0*(\d+)$/i);
        var exactIdHit = false;
        if (idm) {
          var wantPrefix = idm[1] ? idm[1].toLowerCase() : '';
          var wantNum = idm[2];
          for (var k = 0; k < entries.length && acMatches.length < AC_DROPDOWN_MAX; k++) {
            var ek = entries[k];
            if (!ek || !ek.id) continue;
            var eim = String(ek.id).toLowerCase().match(/^(q|st)0*(\d+)$/);
            if (!eim) continue;
            if (wantPrefix && eim[1] !== wantPrefix) continue;
            if (eim[2] !== wantNum) continue;
            acMatches.push(ek);
            acSeen[ek.id] = 1;
            exactIdHit = true;
          }
        }
        var allTextMatches = [];
        if (!exactIdHit) {
          for (var i = 0; i < entries.length; i++) {
            var e = entries[i];
            if (e && e.id && !acSeen[e.id] && entryMatchesSearch(e, q)) allTextMatches.push(e);
          }
        }
        acMatches = acMatches.concat(allTextMatches.slice(0, Math.max(0, AC_DROPDOWN_MAX - acMatches.length)));
        if (!acMatches.length) { acHide(); return; }
        var totalTextMatches = exactIdHit ? acMatches.length : allTextMatches.length;
        var acHtml = acMatches.map(function(e, idx){
          var qHtml = highlightQuestionText(e.question, q);
          return '<div class="q-ac-item" role="option" id="q-ac-opt-' + idx + '"'
            + ' data-ac-idx="' + idx + '">'
            + '<span class="q-ac-id">' + escTxt(e.id) + '</span>'
            + qHtml + '</div>';
        }).join('');
        if (!exactIdHit && totalTextMatches > acMatches.length) {
          acHtml += '<div class="q-ac-more" role="presentation">+'
            + (totalTextMatches - acMatches.length).toLocaleString()
            + ' more in the list below — scroll</div>';
        }
        acBox.innerHTML = acHtml;
        acIndex = -1;
        acBox.classList.add('is-open');
        searchEl.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
        // t169 — exact id match: pre-highlight row 0 so Enter opens it.
        if (exactIdHit) { acSetActive(0); }
        else { searchEl.removeAttribute('aria-activedescendant'); }
      }
      // expose so the shared input handler above can call it
      window.__knAcRefresh = acRefresh;

      // mousedown (not click) so the selection fires before the input's blur
      acBox.addEventListener('mousedown', function(ev){
        var row = ev.target.closest && ev.target.closest('.q-ac-item');
        if (!row) return;
        ev.preventDefault();
        var idx = parseInt(row.getAttribute('data-ac-idx'), 10);
        if (idx >= 0 && idx < acMatches.length) acGo(acMatches[idx]);
      });

      // Escape both closes the dropdown and (via the page's existing
      // capture-phase handler on #q-search) clears the box — standard search
      // UX. acHide() here is harmless either way; when the box clears, the
      // input event also re-runs acRefresh() which hides an empty dropdown.
      searchEl.addEventListener('keydown', function(ev){
        if (!acBox.classList.contains('is-open')) return;
        if (ev.key === 'ArrowDown') {
          ev.preventDefault();
          acSetActive(acIndex + 1 >= acMatches.length ? 0 : acIndex + 1);
        } else if (ev.key === 'ArrowUp') {
          ev.preventDefault();
          acSetActive(acIndex - 1 < 0 ? acMatches.length - 1 : acIndex - 1);
        } else if (ev.key === 'Enter') {
          if (acIndex >= 0 && acIndex < acMatches.length) {
            ev.preventDefault();
            acGo(acMatches[acIndex]);
          }
        } else if (ev.key === 'Escape') {
          acHide();
        }
      });

      searchEl.addEventListener('focus', function(){
        if ((searchEl.value || '').trim()) acRefresh();
      });
      // blur with a small delay so a row mousedown still registers
      searchEl.addEventListener('blur', function(){
        setTimeout(acHide, 120);
      });
    })();
    // Lightweight shim so the shared input handler can call acRefresh even
    // though it lives in a nested closure above.
    function acRefresh(){ if (typeof window.__knAcRefresh === 'function') window.__knAcRefresh(); }

    // t132 — transient "Page N / M" toast for keyboard page-nav ([ ] / Home /
    // End). The [ ] / Home / End handlers smooth-scroll to the top of the
    // viewport, so the bottom pager (the only on-screen "Page N / M" readout)
    // is never visible at the moment you land — keyboard page-nav gave zero
    // confirmation of WHERE you arrived. This pulses a small pill bottom-center
    // for ~1.1s using the exact pattern as the t108 min-qs toast. Reads the
    // post-clamp page geometry paint() publishes to window.__libCurrentPage /
    // window.__libTotalPages, so it always reflects the real clamped page
    // (e.g. pressing End on a 70-page grid shows "Page 70 / 70", and pressing
    // ] on the last page is a no-op that simply re-confirms the current page).
    function showPageToast(){
      try {
        var cur = window.__libCurrentPage || 1;
        var tot = window.__libTotalPages || 1;
        var pt = document.getElementById('libpage-toast');
        if (!pt) {
          pt = document.createElement('div');
          pt.id = 'libpage-toast';
          pt.setAttribute('role', 'status');
          pt.setAttribute('aria-live', 'polite');
          pt.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;left:50%;bottom:78px;transform:translateX(-50%);background:linear-gradient(135deg,rgba(232,113,10,0.95),rgba(184,80,30,0.92));color:#fff;padding:8px 16px 9px;border-radius:14px;font:800 0.74rem ui-monospace,Menlo,Consolas,monospace;letter-spacing:0.08em;box-shadow:0 6px 20px rgba(0,0,0,0.45);z-index:9999;pointer-events:none;opacity:0;transition:opacity .18s ease;text-align:center;';
          // t140 — label line + a thin progress track underneath it. The
          // track is a permanent child of the toast (created once here,
          // reused thereafter — no extra DOM on cold load, same as the
          // toast itself), and only its inner fill width changes per call.
          var lbl = document.createElement('div');
          lbl.id = 'libpage-toast-label';
          var track = document.createElement('div');
          track.style.cssText = 'margin-top:6px;height:3px;border-radius:99px;background:rgba(0,0,0,0.32);overflow:hidden;';
          var fill = document.createElement('div');
          fill.id = 'libpage-toast-fill';
          fill.style.cssText = 'height:100%;width:0%;border-radius:99px;background:rgba(255,255,255,0.92);transition:width .28s cubic-bezier(.4,0,.2,1);';
          track.appendChild(fill);
          pt.appendChild(lbl);
          pt.appendChild(track);
        }
        // t140 — visualize how deep into the library this page sits, so a
        // 10-page '}' jump or an 'End' press lands with an at-a-glance sense
        // of position, not just a number. pct uses the post-clamp geometry
        // paint() publishes; tot===1 grids harmlessly show a full bar.
        var pct = tot > 1 ? Math.round(((cur - 1) / (tot - 1)) * 100) : 100;
        document.getElementById('libpage-toast-label').textContent = '📄 Page ' + cur + ' / ' + tot;
        document.getElementById('libpage-toast-fill').style.width = pct + '%';
        pt.style.opacity = '1';
        clearTimeout(window.__libPageToastT);
        window.__libPageToastT = setTimeout(function(){ pt.style.opacity = '0'; }, 1100);
      } catch(_e){}
    }

    // t70 — '[' and ']' keyboard prev/next page navigation. Mirrors the j/k
    // card-nav pattern (t32). Ignored when typing or holding modifier keys.
    document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e){
      if (e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey || e.altKey) return;
      var t = e.target, tag = (t && t.tagName || '').toUpperCase();
      if (tag === 'INPUT' || tag === 'TEXTAREA' || (t && t.isContentEditable)) return;
      if (e.key === ']') {
        e.preventDefault();
        currentPage = currentPage + 1; // paint() clamps to totalPages
        paint();
        showPageToast();
        window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
      } else if (e.key === '[') {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (currentPage > 1) currentPage = currentPage - 1;
        paint();
        showPageToast();
        window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
      } else if (e.key === '}') {
        // t136 — Shift+] jumps FORWARD 10 pages at once. The library is now
        // ~70 pages; single-step ']' is a slog for deep navigation. Reuses the
        // exact ']' clamp trick — overshoot is harmless, paint() clamps to
        // totalPages (so '}' near the end lands on the last page).
        e.preventDefault();
        currentPage = currentPage + 10; // paint() clamps to totalPages
        paint();
        showPageToast();
        window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
      } else if (e.key === '{') {
        // t136 — Shift+[ jumps BACK 10 pages at once. Floor-clamped to page 1
        // here (paint() only clamps the upper bound), so '{' near the start
        // harmlessly lands on page 1.
        e.preventDefault();
        currentPage = Math.max(1, currentPage - 10);
        paint();
        showPageToast();
        window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
      } else if (e.key === 'Home') {
        // t128 — jump straight to the FIRST page of the grid. Completes the
        // [ ] page-nav pattern (t70) with first/last endpoints. Already-on-page-1
        // is a harmless no-op (paint() re-clamps). No modifier required.
        e.preventDefault();
        currentPage = 1;
        paint();
        showPageToast();
        window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
      } else if (e.key === 'End') {
        // t128 — jump straight to the LAST page. Reuses the same clamp trick the
        // ']' handler relies on: set currentPage above the max and paint()
        // clamps it down to totalPages, so no totalPages reference is needed
        // in this scope (it lives inside paint()).
        e.preventDefault();
        currentPage = 1e9; // paint() clamps to totalPages
        paint();
        showPageToast();
        window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
      } else if (e.key === 's' || e.key === 'S') {
        // t89 — toggle ⭐ Starred-only filter. The state lives inside the
        // refreshStarPill IIFE (line ~1736), so route through a synthetic
        // click on #starred-filter to flip it through the existing handler.
        var sbtn = document.getElementById('starred-filter');
        if (sbtn && sbtn.offsetParent !== null) {
          e.preventDefault();
          currentPage = 1;
          sbtn.click();
        }
      } else if (/^[0-5]$/.test(e.key) && !e.shiftKey) {
        // t108 — Number keys 1-5 set a min-quality-score filter.
        //   1 → qs≥6   2 → qs≥7   3 → qs≥8   4 → qs≥9   5 → qs=10
        //   0 → clear the min-qs filter.
        // Mirrors g/y/s — layers cleanly with every other filter pill and
        // re-paginates to page 1. Result-count line shows "qs ≥ N" so the
        // active threshold is visible. Power-user shortcut for the
        // keyboard culture; mobile users won't trigger it. Hidden when
        // typing (already guarded by the outer isTyping check above).
        e.preventDefault();
        var k = parseInt(e.key, 10);
        var nextMin = k === 0 ? 0 : (k === 5 ? 10 : k + 5);
        // Toggle off if pressing the same threshold a second time.
        if (window.__minQs === nextMin) nextMin = 0;
        window.__minQs = nextMin;
        currentPage = 1;
        paint();
        // Tiny floating toast confirms the new threshold for ~1.2s.
        try {
          var tt = document.getElementById('minqs-toast');
          if (!tt) {
            tt = document.createElement('div');
            tt.id = 'minqs-toast';
            tt.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;left:50%;bottom:78px;transform:translateX(-50%);background:linear-gradient(135deg,rgba(232,113,10,0.95),rgba(184,80,30,0.92));color:#fff;padding:8px 16px;border-radius:99px;font:800 0.74rem ui-monospace,Menlo,Consolas,monospace;letter-spacing:0.08em;box-shadow:0 6px 20px rgba(0,0,0,0.45);z-index:9999;pointer-events:none;opacity:0;transition:opacity .18s ease;';
            document.body.appendChild(tt);
          }
          tt.textContent = nextMin
            ? (nextMin === 10 ? '★ qs = 10 only' : '★ qs ≥ ' + nextMin)
            : '★ qs filter off';
          tt.style.opacity = '1';
          clearTimeout(window.__minQsToastT);
          window.__minQsToastT = setTimeout(function(){ tt.style.opacity = '0'; }, 1200);
        } catch(_e){}
      }
    });

    // Inject JSON-LD: FAQPage (the page itself is a Q/A repository) +
    // TechArticle entries (each library entry is a deep technical answer).
    // Both schemas trigger different rich-result types in Google + Perplexity.
    function injectSchema(entryList) {
      var existing = document.getElementById('ld-qa-schema');
      if (existing) existing.parentNode.removeChild(existing);
      if (!entryList || !entryList.length) return;
      var top = entryList.slice(0, 30);
      var faqMain = top.map(function(e){
        return {
          "@type": "Question",
          "name": e.question,
          "acceptedAnswer": {
            "@type": "Answer",
            "text": "An autonomously researched answer with cited sources. Topics: " + (e.tags || []).join(', ') + ". Read the full answer in the Pulse Knowledge Library.",
            "url": "https://pulserevops.com/knowledge.html#" + e.id
          }
        };
      });
      var techArticles = top.slice(0, 12).map(function(e){
        return {
          "@type": "TechArticle",
          "headline": e.question,
          "url": "https://pulserevops.com/knowledge.html#" + e.id,
          "datePublished": new Date(e.ts).toISOString(),
          "dateModified":  new Date(e.ts).toISOString(),
          "keywords": (e.tags || []).join(', '),
          "author": { "@type": "Organization", "name": "Pulse RevOps · The Machine", "url": "https://pulserevops.com" },
          "publisher": { "@type": "Organization", "name": "Pulse RevOps", "url": "https://pulserevops.com" },
          "mainEntityOfPage": { "@type": "WebPage", "@id": "https://pulserevops.com/knowledge.html#" + e.id }
        };
      });
      var ld = {
        "@context": "https://schema.org",
        "@graph": [
          {
            "@type": "FAQPage",
            "url": "https://pulserevops.com/knowledge.html",
            "name": "Pulse Knowledge Library — sales / GTM / leadership Q&A",
            "mainEntity": faqMain
          }
        ].concat(techArticles)
      };
      var s = document.createElement('script');
      s.type = 'application/ld+json';
      s.id = 'ld-qa-schema';
      s.textContent = JSON.stringify(ld);
      document.head.appendChild(s);

      // Inject ItemList schema enumerating up to 200 entries with positions.
      // Gives Google a structured "collection page" signal so the hub ranks
      // as a curated index instead of a generic content wall.
      var listEl = document.getElementById('ld-itemlist');
      if (listEl) {
        var sortedForList = entryList.slice().sort(function(a, b){
          var an = /^q(\d+)$/.exec(a.id || '');
          var bn = /^q(\d+)$/.exec(b.id || '');
          if (an && bn) return parseInt(bn[1], 10) - parseInt(an[1], 10);
          return (b.ts || 0) - (a.ts || 0);
        }).slice(0, 200);
        var itemList = {
          "@context": "https://schema.org",
          "@type": "ItemList",
          "name": "Pulse Knowledge Library — top entries",
          "url": "https://pulserevops.com/knowledge.html",
          "numberOfItems": sortedForList.length,
          "itemListOrder": "https://schema.org/ItemListOrderDescending",
          "itemListElement": sortedForList.map(function(e, i){
            return {
              "@type": "ListItem",
              "position": i + 1,
              "url": "https://pulserevops.com/knowledge/" + e.id,
              "name": e.question
            };
          })
        };
        listEl.textContent = JSON.stringify(itemList);
      }
    }

    // Find 3 related entries by tag-overlap score — used for the Deep Dive
    // cluster-link section appended to each expanded answer.
    function findRelated(entry, allEntries, n) {
      n = n || 3;
      var myTags = new Set(entry.tags || []);
      if (!myTags.size) return [];
      var scored = allEntries
        .filter(function(e){ return e.id !== entry.id; })
        .map(function(e){
          var overlap = 0;
          (e.tags || []).forEach(function(t){ if (myTags.has(t)) overlap++; });
          return { e: e, score: overlap };
        })
        .filter(function(x){ return x.score > 0; })
        .sort(function(a, b){ return b.score - a.score || b.e.ts - a.e.ts; });
      return scored.slice(0, n).map(function(x){ return x.e; });
    }

    function newestPublishedEntry(list) {
      var best = null;
      var bestNum = 0;
      for (var i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
        var e = list[i];
        if (!e || !e.id || !/^q\d+$/i.test(e.id)) continue;
        if (e.pending || e.source === 'visitor') continue;
        var n = entryQNum(e);
        if (!best || n > bestNum || (n === bestNum && (e.ts || 0) > (best.ts || 0))) {
          best = e;
          bestNum = n;
        }
      }
      return best;
    }
    function showNewAnswerToast(entry) {
      if (!entry || !entry.id) return;
      var el = document.getElementById('kn-new-answer-toast');
      if (!el) {
        el = document.createElement('div');
        el.id = 'kn-new-answer-toast';
        el.setAttribute('role', 'status');
        el.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;left:50%;top:72px;transform:translateX(-50%);z-index:9999;max-width:min(92vw,520px);padding:12px 18px;border-radius:12px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,rgba(255,140,26,0.96),rgba(232,113,10,0.92));color:#0a0a0a;font:800 0.78rem/1.35 inherit;letter-spacing:0.04em;box-shadow:0 8px 28px rgba(0,0,0,0.45);opacity:0;transition:opacity .2s ease;pointer-events:none;text-align:center;';
        document.body.appendChild(el);
      }
      var q = String(entry.question || '').slice(0, 72);
      if (q.length < String(entry.question || '').length) q += '…';
      el.textContent = 'New answer at top · ' + entry.id + ' · ' + q;
      el.style.opacity = '1';
      clearTimeout(showNewAnswerToast._t);
      showNewAnswerToast._t = setTimeout(function(){ el.style.opacity = '0'; }, 4200);
    }

    // Load entries — initial paint, then refresh every 60s so new posts
    // appear without the visitor having to reload the page.
    function loadEntries() {
      return fetch('/.netlify/functions/pulse-machine-library-list?knowledge=1', { cache: 'no-store' })
        .then(function(r){ return r.ok ? r.json() : null; })
        .then(function(d){
          if (d && Array.isArray(d.entries)) {
            // Knowledge library hides sales-training AND industry-kpi entries —
            // they live on their own pillar pages at /sales-trainings and
            // /industry-kpis (different URL space, different canonical, different
            // audience). Filter is content-driven so even legacy entries without
            // the tag get caught via the st-/ik-ID patterns.
            var prevNewestId = window.__libNewestPublishedId || '';
            entries = d.entries.filter(function(e){
              if (!e || !e.id) return false;
              if (/^st\d+$/i.test(e.id)) return false;
              if (/^ik\d+$/i.test(e.id)) return false;
              if (Array.isArray(e.tags) && e.tags.indexOf('sales-training') !== -1) return false;
              if (Array.isArray(e.tags) && e.tags.indexOf('industry-kpi') !== -1) return false;
              return true;
            });
            var newest = newestPublishedEntry(entries);
            if (newest) {
              var grew = prevNewestId && newest.id !== prevNewestId && entryQNum(newest) > entryQNum({ id: prevNewestId });
              window.__libNewestPublishedId = newest.id;
              if (grew && !searchEl.value && !activeTag && !showStarredOnly && !(parseInt(window.__minQs, 10) >= 6)) {
                currentPage = 1;
                showNewAnswerToast(newest);
                try { libEl.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' }); } catch (_s) {}
              }
            }
          }
          injectSchema(entries);
          // t165 — publish the loaded-entry count for the audit-coverage bar
          // (its ?status=1 poll lives in a separate IIFE and has no direct
          // access to this `entries` array).
          window.__libTotalEntries = entries.length;
          // t167 — also publish the gold-verified count. The audit-coverage
          // bar counts an entry as "quality-verified" if it is gold-format
          // (format_v === "2026-05") OR has been error-audited; gold coverage
          // is the dominant signal. Repaint the bar now that entries (and so
          // the gold count) are available, since they can land after the
          // ?status=1 poll's first tick.
          window.__libGoldEntries = entries.filter(function(e){
            return e && e.format_v === '2026-05';
          }).length;
          if (typeof window.__paintAuditCoverage === 'function') {
            window.__paintAuditCoverage();
          }
          if (statTotal && entries.length) statTotal.textContent = entries.length.toLocaleString();
          var jump = document.getElementById('kn-jump-grid');
          if (jump && entries.length) {
            jump.textContent = 'browse ' + entries.length.toLocaleString() + ' answers ↓';
          }
          paint();
          renderTop10();   // t166 — entries now carry question text for the widget
        })
        .catch(function(){ paint(); });
    }
    // Fetch 7-day read counts for every entry → drives reads-badge on every grid card.
    function loadReadCounts() {
      fetch('/.netlify/functions/entry-view?all=1', { cache: 'no-store' })
        .then(function(r){ return r.ok ? r.json() : null; })
        .then(function(d){
          if (d && d.ok && d.counts) {
            readCounts = d.counts;
            if (d.totals) viewTotals = d.totals;
            paint();
            renderTop10();   // t166 — populate the Top-10 widget once reads land
          }
        })
        .catch(function(){ /* silent */ });
    }
    function scrollToPinnedEntry() {
      var pin = window.__pinEntryId;
      if (!pin) return;
      var el = document.querySelector('.entry[data-id="' + pin + '"]');
      if (!el) return;
      el.classList.add('entry-pin-flash');
      el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' });
      setTimeout(function(){ el.classList.remove('entry-pin-flash'); }, 8000);
      try { sessionStorage.removeItem('pulse_pin_entry'); } catch (_e) {}
    }
    loadEntries().then(function(){
      loadReadCounts();
      loadTop10Weekly();
      setTimeout(scrollToPinnedEntry, 120);
    });
    setInterval(function(){ loadEntries(); }, 45000);
    setInterval(loadTop10Weekly, 3600000);
  })();
  </script>

  <script>
    /* Matrix code rain — barely-there green characters falling behind content.
       Runs at low FPS (15) to stay light. Pauses when tab hidden. */
    (function(){
      var canvas = document.getElementById('matrix-rain');
      if (!canvas) return;
      var ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
      var fontSize = 14;
      var cols = 0, drops = [];
      var GLYPHS = '01アイウエオカキクケコサシスセソタチツテトナニヌネノ#%&@$';
      function resize() {
        canvas.width = window.innerWidth;
        canvas.height = window.innerHeight;
        cols = Math.floor(canvas.width / fontSize);
        drops = new Array(cols).fill(0).map(function(){ return Math.random() * -50; });
      }
      function frame() {
        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(7, 10, 15, 0.10)';
        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
        ctx.fillStyle = '#00ff41';
        ctx.font = fontSize + 'px JetBrains Mono, Consolas, monospace';
        for (var i = 0; i < drops.length; i++) {
          var ch = GLYPHS.charAt(Math.floor(Math.random() * GLYPHS.length));
          ctx.fillText(ch, i * fontSize, drops[i] * fontSize);
          if (drops[i] * fontSize > canvas.height && Math.random() > 0.975) drops[i] = 0;
          drops[i] += 0.42;
        }
      }
      resize();
      window.addEventListener('resize', resize);
      var rafId = null, intervalId = null;
      function start() {
        if (intervalId) return;
        intervalId = setInterval(frame, 66); // ~15 fps
      }
      function stop() {
        if (intervalId) { clearInterval(intervalId); intervalId = null; }
      }
      start();
      document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', function(){
        document.hidden ? stop() : start();
      });
    })();

    /* Live entry count for the status pill — same endpoint as the Machine
       room's matrix portal, refreshed every 60s. */
    (function(){
      var el = document.getElementById('ms-count');
      if (!el) return;
      function refresh(){
        fetch('/.netlify/functions/pulse-machine-library-list?status=1', { cache: 'no-store' })
          .then(function(r){ return r.ok ? r.json() : null; })
          .then(function(d){
            if (!d) return;
            if (typeof d.library_total === 'number') {
              var displayN = d.library_total.toLocaleString('en-US');
              el.textContent = displayN;
              var tot = document.getElementById('stat-total');
              if (tot) tot.textContent = displayN;
            }
            if (typeof d.added_today === 'number') {
              var todayEl = document.getElementById('stat-today');
              if (todayEl) todayEl.textContent = d.added_today;
            }
            if (typeof d.run_rate_per_hour === 'number') {
              var rateEl = document.getElementById('stat-rate');
              if (rateEl) rateEl.textContent = d.run_rate_per_hour.toFixed(1);
            }
          })
          .catch(function(){});
      }
      refresh();
      setInterval(refresh, 60000);
    })();
  </script>

      <h3 style="margin:0 0 12px;font-size:1.1rem;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:0.01em;color:#FFE34F;">What does the score mean?</h3>
      <p style="margin:0 0 14px;font-size:0.85rem;line-height:1.55;color:rgba(237,229,216,0.85);">Every entry shows a live, honest <strong>x/10</strong> score. Fresh entries start at <strong>5/10</strong> — schema-complete with an opinionated framework. They climb as the AI does real research and verification work between posts.</p>
      <ul style="margin:0 0 16px;padding-left:18px;font-size:0.82rem;line-height:1.6;color:rgba(237,229,216,0.78);">

        <li><strong>6/10</strong> — every claim resolves to a public source URL.</li>
        <li><strong>7/10</strong> — illustrative numbers replaced with current verified figures from primary sources (10-Qs, press releases, Gartner/Forrester).</li>
        <li><strong>8/10</strong> — adversarial counter-argument section added; alternative views represented honestly.</li>
        <li><strong>9/10</strong> — cross-links to 4+ topically related entries in the library; no internal contradictions.</li>
        <li><strong>10/10</strong> — comprehensive fact-check passed. The answer would survive expert review and is the best response we can produce regardless of where you shopped it.</li>
      </ul>
      <p style="margin:0;font-size:0.72rem;color:rgba(237,229,216,0.55);font-style:italic;">Score is dynamic — entries improve over hours/days/weeks as the polish loop earns each step. Nothing claims 10/10 unless it actually passes every check.</p>

    window.showIQHelp = function() {
      var m = document.getElementById('iq-help-modal');
      if (m) { m.style.display = 'flex'; document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden'; }
    };
    window.hideIQHelp = function() {
      var m = document.getElementById('iq-help-modal');
      if (m) { m.style.display = 'none'; document.body.style.overflow = ''; }
    };
    document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e){
      if (e.key === 'Escape') window.hideIQHelp();
    });
