  <script>
    (function(){
      var modal = document.getElementById('kbd-help-modal');
      if (!modal) return;
      function show(){ modal.style.display = 'flex'; document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden'; }
      function hide(){ modal.style.display = 'none'; document.body.style.overflow = ''; }
      function isTyping(e){
        var t = e.target, tag = (t && t.tagName || '').toUpperCase();
        return tag === 'INPUT' || tag === 'TEXTAREA' || (t && t.isContentEditable);
      }
      document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e){
        if (e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey || e.altKey) return;
        if (e.key === '?' && !isTyping(e)) { e.preventDefault(); show(); return; }
        if (e.key === 'Escape' && modal.style.display === 'flex') { e.preventDefault(); hide(); }
      });
      modal.addEventListener('click', function(e){ if (e.target === modal) hide(); });
      var x = document.getElementById('kbd-help-close');
      if (x) x.addEventListener('click', hide);
    })();
  </script>

  <!-- ─── t156 — BOLD ENERGIZED FLOATING "SCROLL TO TOP" CONTROL ──────────────
       The t83 floating ↑Top button was a tame, off-theme afterthought: a small
       46px circle filled with a generic pink→amber gradient (#ff6b6b, nowhere
       in the pulse palette) and a near-invisible rgba(0,0,0,.18) shadow — it
       sat dead-still in the bottom-right corner and was easy to scroll right
       past on the 1,740+-entry grid. This re-skins that EXISTING button into
       an unmistakable energized launch control, fully on-theme:
         • bigger (54px) pulse-orange→gold gem face with a glassy 3D inset
           highlight and a strong drop shadow so it reads as a real object;
         • a continuously-rotating conic pulse-orange/gold GLOW RING orbits
           just behind it, paired with a slow breathing orange halo, so the
           control is visibly ALIVE even at rest;
         • the ↑ glyph perpetually bobs up-and-down like it is straining to
           launch, with a soft glowing trail beneath it;
         • when it appears (scroll > 800px) it POPS in with an overshoot;
         • HOVER ignites it — the gem lifts and scales, the ring brightens and
           spins faster, the halo flares wide, and the ↑ glyph snaps upward —
           and click/active fires a quick downward press.
       Purely additive + CSS-only: it targets the EXISTING `#lib-top-btn`
       element and only LAYERS on a pseudo-element ring + halo (no markup, no
       JS, no network change — the t83 show/hide + smooth-scroll handler and
       the `t` keybind are all untouched). No layout shift (the ring is an
       ::before underlay, all motion is transform/shadow only). Respects
       prefers-reduced-motion (ring/halo/bob stilled, static legible gem) and
       is fully hidden on print. -->
  <style>
    /* t163 — scroll-to-top button kept on-brand, but the rotating conic glow
       ring, the breathing halo, the pop-in, and the perpetual float are all
       removed. Clean static gem with a calm hover lift. */
    #lib-top-btn{
      width:50px !important; height:50px !important;
      bottom:26px !important; right:26px !important;
      background:linear-gradient(150deg,#FF8C1A 0%,#E8710A 55%,#FFD740 100%) !important;
      color:#160c02 !important;
      font-size:24px !important; font-weight:900 !important;
      border:1px solid rgba(255,215,64,0.55) !important;
      box-shadow:0 6px 18px rgba(0,0,0,0.5),
                 inset 0 1px 2px rgba(255,255,255,0.25) !important;
      transition:transform .16s cubic-bezier(0.22,1,0.36,1),
                 box-shadow .16s ease !important;
    }
    #lib-top-btn:hover{
      transform:translateY(-3px) !important;
      box-shadow:0 12px 28px rgba(0,0,0,0.55),
                 0 0 14px rgba(255,140,26,0.35),
                 inset 0 1px 2px rgba(255,255,255,0.3) !important;
    }
    #lib-top-btn:active{ transform:translateY(0) !important; }
    @media (prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){
      #lib-top-btn:hover{ transform:none !important; }
    }
  </style>
  <button id="lib-top-btn" aria-label="Scroll to top" title="Top of page (or press t)">↑</button>
  <script>
    (function(){
      var btn = document.getElementById('lib-top-btn');
      if (!btn) return;
      function isTyping(e){ var t=(e&&e.target)||document.activeElement; if(!t)return false; var tag=(t.tagName||'').toLowerCase(); return tag==='input'||tag==='textarea'||t.isContentEditable; }
      function refresh(){ btn.style.display = (window.scrollY > 800) ? 'flex' : 'none'; }
      window.addEventListener('scroll', refresh, { passive: true });
      btn.addEventListener('click', function(){ window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' }); });
      window.addEventListener('keydown', function(e){
        if (e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey || e.altKey) return;
        if ((e.key === 't' || e.key === 'T') && !isTyping(e)) { e.preventDefault(); window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' }); }
      });
      refresh();
    })();
  </script>
  <!-- t84 — `c` keyboard shortcut clears ALL active filters at once. -->
  <script>
    (function(){
      function isTyping(e){ var t=(e&&e.target)||document.activeElement; if(!t)return false; var tag=(t.tagName||'').toLowerCase(); return tag==='input'||tag==='textarea'||t.isContentEditable; }
      function clearAll(){
        var sb = document.getElementById('q-search'); if (sb) { sb.value = ''; }
        window.__activeTag = null;
        window.__showStarredOnly = false;
        window.__minQs = 0; // t108 — also clear the min-qs threshold filter.
        window.__page = 1;
        try {
          // Reset filter pill visual states if their elements exist
          var ids = ['filter-starred','filter-today','filter-gold'];
          ids.forEach(function(id){
            var el = document.getElementById(id);
            if (el && el.classList) el.classList.remove('active');
          });
          // Clear any active tag chip
          var chips = document.querySelectorAll('.tag-chip.active, .active-tag');
          chips.forEach(function(c){ if (c.classList) c.classList.remove('active'); });
        } catch(_e) {}
        if (typeof window.paint === 'function') window.paint();
      }
      window.addEventListener('keydown', function(e){
        if (e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey || e.altKey) return;
        if ((e.key === 'c' || e.key === 'C') && !isTyping(e)) { e.preventDefault(); clearAll(); }
      });
    })();

    // t124 — Rung-bonus fetcher. Pulls TODAY's polish events from activity feed
    // and sets window.__pulseRungBonusPct = (rung walks * 0.1) so the verified
    // bar reflects per-rung progress visually for THIS DAY. Refreshes 60s.
    // Scoped to today-only so historical polish stacks don't push the bar to
    // 100% — it resets at local midnight and shows fresh per-day momentum.
    // Hard-capped at 5pp on top of binary verified % to prevent runaway.
    (function(){
      function refreshRungBonus() {
        fetch('/.netlify/functions/pulse-activity-feed?day=today&limit=2000', { cache: 'no-store' })
          .then(function(r){ return r.ok ? r.json() : null; })
          .then(function(data){
            if (!data || !data.events) return;
            var rungCount = data.events.filter(function(e){ return e.type === 'polish'; }).length;
            window.__pulseRungBonusPct = Math.min(5, rungCount * 0.1);
            if (typeof window.paint === 'function') window.paint();
          })
          .catch(function(){});
      }
      refreshRungBonus();
      setInterval(refreshRungBonus, 60000);
    })();
  </script>

  .pu-row[data-surface="salestraining"] .pu-marker{ background:#A78BFA; }
  .pu-row[data-surface="salestraining"] .pu-tag  { background:rgba(167,139,250,0.18); color:#C7B6FF; }
  .pu-row[data-surface="critter"]    .pu-marker{ background:#2DD4BF; }
  .pu-row[data-surface="critter"]    .pu-tag   { background:rgba(45,212,191,0.18); color:#5EEAD4; }
  .pu-row[data-surface="livemap"]    .pu-marker{ background:#2DD4BF; }
  .pu-row[data-surface="livemap"]    .pu-tag   { background:rgba(45,212,191,0.18); color:#5EEAD4; }
  .pu-row[data-surface="polish"]     .pu-marker{ background:#FF7A59; }

  .pu-row[data-surface="goldformat"] .pu-marker{ background:#FFD740; }
  .pu-row[data-surface="goldformat"] .pu-tag   { background:rgba(255,215,64,0.18); color:#FFD740; }
  .pu-row[data-surface="addnew"]     .pu-marker{ background:#E8710A; }

  .pu-row[data-surface="other"]      .pu-tag   { background:rgba(237,229,216,0.08); color:rgba(237,229,216,0.6); }
  #pulse-updates-modal .pu-empty{
    text-align:center; padding:28px 12px; color:rgba(237,229,216,0.4);
    font-size:0.84rem; font-style:italic;
  }
  #pulse-updates-modal .pu-loading{
    text-align:center; padding:28px 12px; color:rgba(237,229,216,0.45);
    font-size:0.8rem;
  }
  #pulse-updates-modal .pu-more-btn{
    background:rgba(232,113,10,0.10); color:#FFA940;
    border:1px solid rgba(232,113,10,0.32); border-radius:100px;
    padding:7px 16px; font-size:0.7rem; font-weight:800;
    letter-spacing:0.08em; text-transform:uppercase; cursor:pointer;
    margin:14px auto 4px; display:block; font-family:inherit;
  }
  #pulse-updates-modal .pu-more-btn:hover{
    background:rgba(232,113,10,0.18); color:#FFD740;
  }
  @media (max-width:720px){
    #pulse-updates-modal{ padding:4vh 12px 12px; }
    #pulse-updates-modal .pu-card{ max-height:92vh; }
    #pulse-updates-modal .pu-rot-strip{ flex-direction:row; }

    #pulse-updates-pill{ bottom:14px; left:14px; font-size:0.72rem; padding:7px 12px 7px 10px; }
  }
  @media print{
    #pulse-updates-pill, #pulse-updates-modal{ display:none !important; }
  }
</style>
<button id="pulse-updates-pill" type="button" title="What The Machine has been shipping (press u)" aria-label="Open updates panel">
  <span class="pu-dot" aria-hidden="true"></span>
  <span>Updates</span>
  <span class="pu-badge" id="pu-pill-count">·</span>
</button>
<div id="pulse-updates-modal" role="dialog" aria-labelledby="pu-modal-title" aria-hidden="true">
  <div class="pu-card" onclick="event.stopPropagation()">
    <div class="pu-head">
      <div id="pu-modal-title" class="pu-title">⚡ The Machine — Live Updates</div>
      <button class="pu-close" type="button" aria-label="Close updates panel">×</button>
    </div>
    <div class="pu-rotation">
      <div class="pu-rot-head">
        <span id="pu-rot-label">v13 · light-only · 30-min cadence</span>
        <span class="pu-burst-pill" id="pu-burst-pill">burst —</span>
      </div>
      <div class="pu-rot-strip" id="pu-rot-strip" aria-label="Rotation tasks"></div>
    </div>
    <div class="pu-feed" id="pu-feed">
      <div class="pu-loading">Loading recent activity…</div>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>
<script>
  (function(){
    var pill   = document.getElementById('pulse-updates-pill');
    var modal  = document.getElementById('pulse-updates-modal');
    var feedEl = document.getElementById('pu-feed');
    var ropt   = document.getElementById('pu-rot-strip');
    var rlbl   = document.getElementById('pu-rot-label');
    var bpill  = document.getElementById('pu-burst-pill');
    var pillCt = document.getElementById('pu-pill-count');
    var closeBtn = modal.querySelector('.pu-close');
    if (!pill || !modal) return;

    var refreshTimer = null;
    var lastFeed = null;
    var lastLoop = null;
    var showAll = false;

    var FALLBACK = {
      tasks: ['library','crm','warroom','critter'],
      next: 0,
      last_tick: 140,
      phase: 'v13-light-only'
    };

    function isTyping(e){
      var t=(e&&e.target)||document.activeElement; if(!t) return false;
      var tag=(t.tagName||'').toLowerCase();
      return tag==='input'||tag==='textarea'||t.isContentEditable;
    }

    function openModal(){
      modal.classList.add('show');
      modal.setAttribute('aria-hidden','false');
      refresh();
      if (refreshTimer) clearInterval(refreshTimer);
      refreshTimer = setInterval(refresh, 60000);
    }
    function closeModal(){
      modal.classList.remove('show');
      modal.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
      if (refreshTimer) { clearInterval(refreshTimer); refreshTimer = null; }
    }
    pill.addEventListener('click', openModal);
    closeBtn.addEventListener('click', closeModal);
    modal.addEventListener('click', function(e){ if (e.target === modal) closeModal(); });
    window.addEventListener('keydown', function(e){
      if (e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey || e.altKey) return;
      if (e.key === 'Escape' && modal.classList.contains('show')) { e.preventDefault(); closeModal(); return; }
      if ((e.key === 'u' || e.key === 'U') && !isTyping(e) && !modal.classList.contains('show')) {
        e.preventDefault(); openModal();
      }
    });

    function fetchLoopState(cb){
      var urls = ['/loop-state.json', '/lab/_improvement_loop_state.json'];
      var idx = 0;
      function tryNext(){
        if (idx >= urls.length){ cb(FALLBACK); return; }
        fetch(urls[idx], { cache:'no-store' })
          .then(function(r){ if (!r.ok) throw new Error('bad'); return r.json(); })
          .then(function(j){ cb(j || FALLBACK); })
          .catch(function(){ idx++; tryNext(); });
      }
      tryNext();
    }

    function paintRotation(state){
      var tasks = (state && state.tasks) || FALLBACK.tasks;
      var next  = (state && typeof state.next === 'number') ? state.next : FALLBACK.next;
      var len   = tasks.length;
      var cur   = ((next - 1) % len + len) % len;
      var n1    = (next % len + len) % len;
      var n2    = ((next + 1) % len + len) % len;
      var n3    = ((next + 2) % len + len) % len;
      ropt.innerHTML = '';
      tasks.forEach(function(t, i){
        var pillEl = document.createElement('span');
        pillEl.className = 'pu-rot-task';
        var st = 'pending';
        if (i === cur) st = 'current';
        else if (i === n1 || i === n2 || i === n3) st = 'next';
        else if (i < cur) st = 'done';
        pillEl.setAttribute('data-state', st);
        pillEl.setAttribute('data-task', t);
        pillEl.textContent = t;
        ropt.appendChild(pillEl);
      });
      var burstPos = ((cur) % len) + 1;
      bpill.textContent = 'burst ' + burstPos + '/' + len;
      var phase = (state && state.phase) || FALLBACK.phase;
      var cad = (state && state.cadence_minutes) || 30;
      rlbl.textContent = phase + ' · ' + cad + '-min cadence';
    }

    function surfaceOf(ev){
      var t = ev.type || '';
      var task = ev.task || '';
      if (task) return task;
      if (t === 'verified' || t === 'polish') return 'polish';
      if (t === 'created') return 'library';
      if (t === 'training-created') return 'salestraining';
      if (t === 'index') return 'other';
      if (t.indexOf('cron-') === 0) return t.replace('cron-','');
      return 'other';
    }
    function timeAgo(ts){
      var s = Math.max(1, Math.floor((Date.now() - ts) / 1000));
      if (s < 60)  return s + 's ago';
      if (s < 3600) return Math.floor(s/60) + 'm ago';
      if (s < 86400) return Math.floor(s/3600) + 'h ago';
      return Math.floor(s/86400) + 'd ago';
    }
    function rowHTML(ev){
      var surface = surfaceOf(ev);
      var idTxt = ev.id ? ('<span class="pu-id">' + escapeHTML(ev.id) + '</span>') : '';
      var tagTxt = ev.label ? ('<span class="pu-tag">' + escapeHTML(surface) + '</span>') : '';
      var title  = ev.title || ev.label || '(no title)';
      var meta   = (ev.label || '') + ' · ' + timeAgo(ev.ts || Date.now());
      var inner = ''
        + '<div class="pu-marker"></div>'
        + '<div class="pu-body">'
        +   tagTxt + idTxt
        +   '<span class="pu-text">' + escapeHTML(title) + '</span>'
        +   '<div class="pu-meta">' + escapeHTML(meta) + '</div>'
        + '</div>';
      if (ev.url) inner = '<a href="' + escapeAttr(ev.url) + '" style="display:flex;gap:12px;align-items:flex-start;flex:1;text-decoration:none;color:inherit;">' + inner + '</a>';
      return '<div class="pu-row" data-surface="' + escapeAttr(surface) + '">' + inner + '</div>';
    }
    function escapeHTML(s){
      return String(s == null ? '' : s)
        .replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;')
        .replace(/"/g,'&quot;').replace(/'/g,'&#39;');
    }
    function escapeAttr(s){ return escapeHTML(s); }

    function paintFeed(data){
      var events = (data && data.events) || [];
      if (!events.length){
        feedEl.innerHTML = '<div class="pu-empty">No activity in the last 24h. The Machine is between ticks.</div>';
        pillCt.textContent = '0';
        return;
      }
      var crons = events.filter(function(e){ return e.type && e.type.indexOf('cron-') === 0; });
      var qWins = events.filter(function(e){ return e.type === 'verified' || (e.type === 'polish' && e.to === 10); });
      var stAdds = events.filter(function(e){ return e.type === 'training-created'; });
      var libAdds = events.filter(function(e){ return e.type === 'created'; });
      var cronShow = crons.slice(0, showAll ? 50 : 18);
      var qShow    = qWins.slice(0, showAll ? 40 : 10);
      var stShow   = stAdds.slice(0, showAll ? 20 : 5);
      var libShow  = libAdds.slice(0, showAll ? 20 : 10);
      var html = '';
      if (cronShow.length){ html += '<div class="pu-section-h">Recent ticks (' + cronShow.length + ')</div>' + cronShow.map(rowHTML).join(''); }
      if (qShow.length){ html += '<div class="pu-section-h">Polish / gold wins (' + qShow.length + ')</div>' + qShow.map(rowHTML).join(''); }
      if (stShow.length){ html += '<div class="pu-section-h">Sales training adds (' + stShow.length + ')</div>' + stShow.map(rowHTML).join(''); }
      if (libShow.length){ html += '<div class="pu-section-h">New library entries (' + libShow.length + ')</div>' + libShow.map(rowHTML).join(''); }
      if (!html){ html = '<div class="pu-empty">Quiet stretch. Check back in a few minutes.</div>'; }
      var totalShown = cronShow.length + qShow.length + stShow.length + libShow.length;
      var totalAvail = crons.length + qWins.length + stAdds.length + libAdds.length;
      if (!showAll && totalAvail > totalShown){
        html += '<button class="pu-more-btn" id="pu-more-btn" type="button">Show more · ' + (totalAvail - totalShown) + ' hidden</button>';
      }
      feedEl.innerHTML = html;
      var mb = document.getElementById('pu-more-btn');
      if (mb) mb.addEventListener('click', function(){ showAll = true; paintFeed(lastFeed); });
      pillCt.textContent = String(events.length > 99 ? '99+' : events.length);
    }

    function fetchFeed(cb){
      var url = '/.netlify/functions/pulse-activity-feed?day=24h&limit=120';
      fetch(url, { cache:'no-store' })
        .then(function(r){ if (!r.ok) throw new Error('bad'); return r.json(); })
        .then(function(j){ cb(j && j.events ? j : { events: [] }); })
        .catch(function(){ cb({ events: [] }); });
    }

    function refresh(){
      fetchLoopState(function(state){ lastLoop = state; paintRotation(state); });
      fetchFeed(function(data){ lastFeed = data; paintFeed(data); });
    }

    fetchFeed(function(data){
      lastFeed = data;
      var n = (data && data.events && data.events.length) || 0;
      pillCt.textContent = n > 99 ? '99+' : (n || '·');
    });
  })();

  // t120 — Sticky filter chip row module. Shows the sticky bar when window.scrollY > 360,
  // two-way syncs the mini search with #q-search, delegates pill clicks to the originals,
  // and mirrors active state + counts via MutationObservers on the source buttons so the
  // sticky bar always reflects single-source-of-truth state set by paint() + filter logic.
  (function(){
    var bar = document.getElementById('kn-sticky-filterbar');
    if (!bar) return;
    var stkSearch  = document.getElementById('kn-stk-search');
    var stkStar    = document.getElementById('kn-stk-starred');
    var stkTop     = document.getElementById('kn-stk-top');
    var stkStarCt  = document.getElementById('kn-stk-starred-count');

    var mainSearch = document.getElementById('q-search');
    var srcStar    = document.getElementById('starred-filter');
    var srcStarCt  = document.getElementById('starred-count');

    // Scroll show/hide — show after ~360px (past hero + main search). rAF-throttled.
    var SHOW_AT = 360;
    var ticking = false;
    var lastShown = false;
    function updateVisible(){
      ticking = false;
      var y = window.pageYOffset || document.documentElement.scrollTop || 0;
      var should = y > SHOW_AT;
      if (should !== lastShown) {
        lastShown = should;
        bar.classList.toggle('show', should);
      }
    }
    window.addEventListener('scroll', function(){
      if (!ticking) { ticking = true; requestAnimationFrame(updateVisible); }
    }, { passive: true });
    window.addEventListener('resize', updateVisible, { passive: true });
    updateVisible();

    // Two-way search sync. Typing in the sticky search updates the main search
    // and dispatches its 'input' event so all existing filter logic fires.
    if (stkSearch && mainSearch) {
      var muted = false;
      stkSearch.addEventListener('input', function(){
        if (muted) return;
        muted = true;
        mainSearch.value = stkSearch.value;
        mainSearch.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
        muted = false;
      });
      // Mirror back: when main search changes (typing in main, or `c` clear,
      // or `/` focus-then-type), reflect it into the sticky input.
      mainSearch.addEventListener('input', function(){
        if (muted) return;
        muted = true;
        if (stkSearch.value !== mainSearch.value) stkSearch.value = mainSearch.value;
        muted = false;
      });
      // Press Esc in the sticky search to blur (matches the main-search UX)
      stkSearch.addEventListener('keydown', function(e){
        if (e.key === 'Escape') {
          stkSearch.value = '';
          mainSearch.value = '';
          mainSearch.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
          stkSearch.blur();
        }
      });
    }

    // Pill click delegation — fire the original button's click so all state lives
    // in one place (no duplicated toggle logic). Sync happens via observers below.
    function delegate(stkBtn, srcBtn){
      if (!stkBtn || !srcBtn) return;
      stkBtn.addEventListener('click', function(e){
        e.preventDefault();
        srcBtn.click();
      });
    }
    delegate(stkStar,  srcStar);

    // ↑ Top — smooth-scroll to top (mirrors the t83 floating button so the
    // sticky chrome row carries the same action without reaching bottom-right).
    if (stkTop) {
      stkTop.addEventListener('click', function(){
        try { window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' }); }
        catch(_) { window.scrollTo(0, 0); }
      });
    }

    // State sync — mirror visible-ness, active style, and count text from each
    // source pill. Uses MutationObserver on style + count text so we don't
    // depend on a paint() hook (which would couple modules). Cheap: only fires
    // when the source button actually mutates.
    function syncPill(stkBtn, srcBtn, stkCt, srcCt){
      if (!stkBtn || !srcBtn) return;
      function apply(){
        var visible = srcBtn.style.display && srcBtn.style.display !== 'none';
        stkBtn.classList.toggle('shown', !!visible);
        // Active detection: the source uses inline gradient backgrounds; when
        // the filter is ON the gradient stops are stronger. Easiest detection:
        // look at srcBtn.style.color — the on-state flips to '#fff' or '#1a1208'
        // (non-default), while off-state keeps '#FFD740'. Fall back to bg check.
        var col = (srcBtn.style.color || '').toLowerCase();
        var on = col === '#fff' || col === 'rgb(255, 255, 255)' || col === '#1a1208' || col === 'rgb(26, 18, 8)';
        stkBtn.classList.toggle('active', !!on);
        if (stkCt && srcCt) stkCt.textContent = srcCt.textContent || '0';
      }
      apply();
      var mo = new MutationObserver(apply);
      mo.observe(srcBtn, { attributes: true, attributeFilter: ['style'] });
      if (srcCt) {
        var mo2 = new MutationObserver(apply);
        mo2.observe(srcCt, { childList: true, characterData: true, subtree: true });
      }
    }
    syncPill(stkStar,  srcStar,  stkStarCt,  srcStarCt);
  })();
</script>
<script>
  /* t152 — Bold scroll-progress bar driver. Sets the #kn-scroll-progress bar
     width to the current scroll depth as a percentage. Single passive,
     rAF-throttled scroll + resize handler so rapid scrolling coalesces to one
     paint per frame; no new DOM beyond the bar already in markup, no network,
     no coupling to paint() or any filter logic. */
  (function(){
    var bar = document.getElementById('kn-scroll-progress');
    if (!bar) return;
    var ticking = false;
    function update(){
      ticking = false;
      var doc = document.documentElement;
      var max = (doc.scrollHeight || 0) - (window.innerHeight || 0);
      var pct = max > 0
        ? Math.min(100, Math.max(0, (window.pageYOffset || doc.scrollTop || 0) / max * 100))
        : 0;
      bar.style.width = pct.toFixed(2) + '%';
    }
    function onScroll(){
      if (ticking) return;
      ticking = true;
      window.requestAnimationFrame(update);
    }
    window.addEventListener('scroll', onScroll, { passive: true });
    window.addEventListener('resize', onScroll, { passive: true });
    update();
  })();
</script>
